{ NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { validateCreditDeductionAmount } from \"./validation\";\n\nimport {\n  deductCreditBalance,\n  findAccountByKey,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * License Credits API\n *\n * Manages credit balance for licensed users.\n * Used by HyperWhisper Cloud (CF Workers) to check balance and deduct credits.\n *\n * ENDPOINTS:\n * - GET: Get credit balance for a license key\n * - POST: Deduct credits (record usage)\n *\n * SECURITY:\n * - The license key itself acts as authentication\n * - Only valid license keys can query/deduct credits\n */\n\n/**\n * GET /api/license/credits\n *\n * Get credit balance for a license key.\n */\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const { searchParams } = new URL(req.url);\n  const licenseKey = searchParams.get(\"license_key\");\n\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits,\n      stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits balance error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Failed to get credit balance\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * POST /api/license/credits\n *\n * Deduct credits from a license (record usage).\n */\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { license_key, amount, metadata } = body;\n\n    if (!license_key || typeof license_key !== \"string\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"license_key is required\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const amountError = validateCreditDeductionAmount(amount);\n\n    if (amountError) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const license = await findAccountByKey(license_key.trim());\n\n    if (!license) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"License key not found\" },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n        { status: 400 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    let newCredits: number;\n\n    try {\n      // Atomic SQL decrement (floored at 0) — concurrent POSTs cannot\n      // double-spend via a read-then-write race.\n      newCredits = await deductCreditBalance(license.userId, amount);\n    } catch (updateError) {\n      console.error(\"Credit deduction failed:\", updateError);\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Failed to deduct credits. Please retry.\" },\n        { status: 409 }\n      );\n    }\n\n    console.log(`Credits deducted: ${amount} from license ${license_key.substring(0, 7)}...`, {\n      remaining: newCredits,\n      metadata,\n    });\n\n    return NextResponse.json({\n      credits_remaining: newCredits,\n      credits_deducted: amount,\n    });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credits deduction error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Failed to deduct credits\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"be86f7ae-9cfc-46a9-a004-7e55be79f458","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

When that secret is unset (the expected state for direct Outrank\n22\t * deliveries) the bearer token is the sole auth — the HMAC branch is then\n23\t * intentionally inert, not dead code, and exists so an integrity-binding proxy\n24\t * can be added later without a code change.\n25\t *\n26\t * Upserts each article into blog_posts (keyed on external_id) and revalidates\n27\t * the blog routes so new posts appear immediately.\n28\t */\n29\t\n30\texport const runtime = \"nodejs\";\n31\texport const dynamic = \"force-dynamic\";\n32\t\n33\t// Reject oversized payloads before buffering them into memory. Outrank batches\n34\t// are small; a few MB is generous headroom for a legitimate publish.\n35\tconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 MB\n36\t// Cap the number of articles processed in a single delivery so one request\n37\t// cannot tie up the function for an unbounded number of serial DB round-trips.\n38\tconst MAX_ARTICLES = 100;\n39\t\n40\ttype OutrankArticle = {\n41\t  id?: string;\n42\t  title?: string;\n43\t  content_markdown?: string;\n44\t  content_html?: string;\n45\t  meta_description?: string;\n46\t  description?: string;\n47\t  created_at?: string;\n48\t  published_at?: string;\n49\t  image_url?: string;\n50\t  image_alt?: string;\n51\t  slug?: string;\n52\t  tags?: string[];\n53\t  url?: string;\n54\t};\n55\t\n56\ttype OutrankPayload = {\n57\t  event_type?: string;\n58\t  timestamp?: string;\n59\t  data?: { articles?: OutrankArticle[] };\n60\t};\n61\t\n62\t/**\n63\t * Verify a per-request HMAC-SHA256 signature over the raw body. Returns false\n64\t * when no signing secret is configured (so the caller can fall back to the\n65\t * static bearer token).\n66\t */\n67\tfunction isSignatureValid(rawBody: string, header: string | null): boolean {\n68\t  const secret = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET;\n69\t  if (!secret || !header) return false;\n70\t\n71\t  // Accept either a bare hex digest or a `sha256=<hex>` prefixed value.\n72\t  const provided = header.startsWith(\"sha256=\")\n73\t    ? header.slice(\"sha256=\".length).trim()\n74\t    : header.trim();\n75\t\n76\t  const expected = createHmac(\"sha256\", secret).update(rawBody).digest(\"hex\");\n77\t\n78\t  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n79\t  const providedBuf = encoder.encode(provided.toLowerCase());\n80\t  const expectedBuf = encoder.encode(expected);\n81\t  if (providedBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length) return false;\n82\t  return timingSafeEqual(providedBuf, expectedBuf);\n83\t}\n84\t\n85\tfunction isBearerAuthorized(req: NextRequest): boolean {\n86\t  const expected = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN;\n87\t  if (!expected) return false;\n88\t  const header = req.headers.get(\"authorization\");\n89\t  if (!header || !header.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) return false;\n90\t  return timingSafeEqualSecret(header.slice(\"Bearer \".length).trim(), expected);\n91\t}\n92\t\n93\tfunction slugify(input: string): string {\n94\t  return input\n95\t    .toString()\n96\t    .toLowerCase()\n97\t    .normalize(\"NFKD\")\n98\t    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, \"\")\n99\t    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n100\t    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, \"\")\n101\t    .slice(0, 120);\n102\t}\n103\t\n104\tconst LOCALE = \"en\";\n105\t\n106\t/**\n107\t * Return true when `slug` is free within the locale, i.e. not already claimed\n108\t * by a DIFFERENT external article. (A row owned by `externalId` itself is fine\n109\t * — the upsert resolves that on the externalId conflict target.)\n110\t */\n111\tasync function isSlugFree(slug: string, externalId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n112\t  const existing = await db\n113\t    .select({ id: blogPosts.id })\n114\t    .from(blogPosts)\n115\t    .where(\n116\t      and(\n117\t        eq(blogPosts.locale, LOCALE),\n118\t        eq(blogPosts.slug, slug),\n119\t        ne(blogPosts.externalId, externalId),\n120\t      ),\n121\t    )\n122\t    .limit(1);\n123\t  return existing.length === 0;\n124\t}\n125\t\n126\t/**\n127\t * Resolve a slug that is unique within the locale. If the desired slug is\n128\t * already taken by a DIFFERENT external article, append a suffix derived from\n129\t * the external id so the new article still persists instead of throwing a\n130\t * (locale, slug) unique violation. The first candidate is verified too — and if\n131\t * it is also taken (e.g. duplicate-title articles whose external ids share the\n132\t * leading 8 alphanumerics) we widen the suffix and finally fall back to the\n133\t * full external id, which is globally unique, so a free slug is guaranteed.\n134\t */\n135\tasync function resolveUniqueSlug(\n136\t  desired: string,\n137\t  externalId: string,\n138\t): Promise<string> {\n139\t  if (await isSlugFree(desired, externalId)) return desired;\n140\t\n141\t  const sanitizedId = externalId.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, \"\").toLowerCase();\n142\t\n143\t  // Try progressively longer slices of the external id, then the full id, so\n144\t  // articles that collide on a short shared prefix still resolve to distinct\n145\t  // slugs. Each candidate is verified before use.\n146\t  const suffixLengths = [8, 16, sanitizedId.length];\n147\t  for (const len of suffixLengths) {\n148\t    const suffix = sanitizedId.slice(0, len);\n149\t    if (!suffix) continue;\n150\t    const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n151\t    const candidate = `${base}-${suffix}`;\n152\t    if (await isSlugFree(candidate, externalId)) return candidate;\n153\t  }\n154\t\n155\t  // Last resort: a timestamp keeps the slug unique even when the external id\n156\t  // alone somehow still collides, so the article persists rather than being\n157\t  // dropped on a unique violation.\n158\t  const suffix = `${sanitizedId || \"post\"}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;\n159\t  const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n160\t  return `${base}-${suffix}`;\n161\t}\n162\t\n163\tfunction escapeHtml(value: string): string {\n164\t  return value\n165\t    .replace(/&/g, \"&amp;\")\n166\t    .replace(/</g, \"&lt;\")\n167\t    .replace(/>/g, \"&gt;\")\n168\t    .replace(/\"/g, \"&quot;\")\n169\t    .replace(/'/g, \"&#39;\");\n170\t}\n171\t\n172\tfunction markdownToHtml(markdown: string): string {\n173\t  return markdown\n174\t    .trim()\n175\t    .split(/\\n{2,}/)\n176\t    .map((block) => {\n177\t      const text = escapeHtml(block.trim());\n178\t      if (!text) return \"\";\n179\t\n180\t      const heading = text.match(/^(#{1,3})\\s+(.+)$/);\n181\t      if (heading) {\n182\t        const level = heading[1].length;\n183\t        return `<h${level}>${heading[2]}</h${level}>`;\n184\t      }\n185\t\n186\t      return `<p>${text.replace(/\\n/g, \"<br />\")}</p>`;\n187\t    })\n188\t    .filter(Boolean)\n189\t    .join(\"\\n\");\n190\t}\n191\t\n192\tfunction parsePublishedAt(article: OutrankArticle): {\n193\t  publishedAt?: Date;\n194\t  error?: string;\n195\t} {\n196\t  const candidate = article.published_at ?? article.created_at;\n197\t  if (!candidate) return {};\n198\t\n199\t  const parsed = new Date(candidate);\n200\t  if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {\n201\t    const field = article.published_at ? \"published_at\" : \"created_at\";\n202\t    return { error: `invalid ${field}: ${candidate}` };\n203\t  }\n204\t\n205\t  return { publishedAt: parsed };\n206\t}\n207\t\n208\tfunction normalizeTags(tags: unknown): string[] {\n209\t  if (!Array.isArray(tags)) return [];\n210\t\n211\t  return tags\n212\t    .filter((tag): tag is string => typeof tag === \"string\")\n213\t    .map((tag) => tag.trim())\n214\t    .filter(Boolean)\n215\t    .slice(0, 12);\n216\t}\n217\t\n218\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n219\t  const contentLength = Number(req.headers.get(\"content-length\"));\n220\t  if (Number.isFinite(contentLength) && contentLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n221\t    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Payload too large\" }, { status: 413 });\n222\t  }\n223\t\n224\t  // Signature verification needs the raw bytes, so we can only HMAC-check after\n225\t  // buffering the body. The bearer token, by contrast, is independent of the\n226\t  // payload — so when HMAC signing is disabled (the normal direct-Outrank\n227\t  // setup) reject a bad bearer token BEFORE allocating the body, instead of\n228\t  // buffering an unsigned, unauthenticated request.\n229\t  const signature =\n230\t    req.headers.get(\"x-outrank-signature\") ??\n231\t    req.headers.get(\"outrank-signature\");\n232\t  const signingEnabled = Boolean(env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET);\n233\t  if (!signingEnabled && !isBearerAuthorized(req)) {\n234\t    return NextResponse.json(\n235\t      { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n236\t      { status: 401 },\n237\t    );\n238\t  }\n239\t\n240\t  // Read the raw body once so we can both size-check it and verify an HMAC\n241\t  // signature over the exact bytes.\n242\t  let rawBody: string;\n243\t  try {\n244\t    rawBody = await req.text();\n245\t  } catch {\n246\t    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid body\" }, { status: 400 });\n247\t  }\n248\t\n249\t  if (Buffer.byteLength(rawBody, \"utf8\") > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n250\t    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Payload too large\" }, { status: 413 });\n251\t  }\n252\t\n253\t  // When signing is enabled, prefer HMAC signature verification but still fall\n254\t  // back to the static bearer token for callers that have not migrated to\n255\t  // signing. (When signing is disabled the bearer token was already verified\n256\t  // above before the body was buffered.)\n257\t  if (signingEnabled) {\n258\t    const authorized =\n259\t      isSignatureValid(rawBody, signature) || isBearerAuthorized(req);\n260\t    if (!authorized) {\n261\t      return NextResponse.json(\n262\t        { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n263\t        { status: 401 },\n264\t      );\n265\t    }\n266\t  }\n267\t\n268\t  let payload: OutrankPayload;\n269\t  try {\n270\t    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody) as OutrankPayload;\n271\t  } catch {\n272\t    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid JSON\" }, { status: 400 });\n273\t  }\n274\t\n275\t  if (payload.event_type !== \"publish_articles\") {\n276\t    return NextResponse.json(\n277\t      { error: `Unsupported event_type: ${payload.event_type}` },\n278\t      { status: 400 },\n279\t    );\n280\t  }\n281\t\n282\t  const articles = payload.data?.articles;\n283\t  if (!Array.isArray(articles) || articles.length === 0) {\n284\t    return NextResponse.json(\n285\t      { error: \"No articles in payload\" },\n286\t      { status: 400 },\n287\t    );\n288\t  }\n289\t\n290\t  if (articles.length > MAX_ARTICLES) {\n291\t    return NextResponse.json(\n292\t      {\n293\t        error: `Too many articles: ${articles.length} (max ${MAX_ARTICLES})`,\n294\t      },\n295\t      { status: 413 },\n296\t    );\n297\t  }\n298\t\n299\t  const processed: Array<{ id: string; slug: string }> = [];\n300\t  const skipped: Array<{ index: number; reason: string }> = [];\n301\t  // Track whether any skip was a TRANSIENT failure (e.g. a DB upsert error)\n302\t  // rather than a permanent validation error. Only transient failures justify\n303\t  // a non-2xx that asks the sender to retry; a payload whose articles are all\n304\t  // permanently invalid must not be retried forever.\n305\t  let hadTransientFailure = false;\n306\t\n307\t  for (let i = 0; i < articles.length; i++) {\n308\t    const article = articles[i];\n309\t    const markdown = article?.content_markdown?.trim() ?? \"\";\n310\t    const html = article?.content_html?.trim() || markdownToHtml(markdown);\n311\t    const description = article?.meta_description ?? article?.description;\n312\t\n313\t    if (!article?.id || !article.title?.trim() || (!markdown && !html)) {\n314\t      skipped.push({\n315\t        index: i,\n316\t        reason: \"missing id/title/content\",\n317\t      });\n318\t      continue;\n319\t    }\n320\t\n321\t    const title = article.title.trim();\n322\t    const slugSource = article.slug?.trim() || title;\n323\t    const baseSlug = slugify(slugSource);\n324\t    if (!baseSlug) {\n325\t      skipped.push({ index: i, reason: \"slug resolved to empty string\" });\n326\t      continue;\n327\t    }\n328\t\n329\t    // Validate published_at/created_at before either reaches the driver: an Invalid Date\n330\t    // makes node-postgres throw RangeError on serialization, which would\n331\t    // otherwise be swallowed as a silent skip.\n332\t    const { publishedAt, error: publishedAtError } = parsePublishedAt(article);\n333\t    if (publishedAtError) {\n334\t      skipped.push({\n335\t        index: i,\n336\t        reason: publishedAtError,\n337\t      });\n338\t      continue;\n339\t    }\n340\t\n341\t    const tags = normalizeTags(article.tags);\n342\t\n343\t    try {\n344\t      // Avoid a (locale, slug) unique violation when a different article has\n345\t      // already claimed this slug.\n346\t      const slug = await resolveUniqueSlug(baseSlug, article.id);\n347\t\n348\t      const [row] = await db\n349\t        .insert(blogPosts)\n350\t        .values({\n351\t          externalId: article.id,\n352\t          source: \"outrank\",\n353\t          locale: LOCALE,\n354\t          slug,\n355\t          title,\n356\t          description: description?.trim() || null,\n357\t          contentMarkdown: markdown,\n358\t          contentHtml: html,\n359\t          imageUrl: article.image_url ?? null,\n360\t          imageAlt: article.image_alt ?? title,\n361\t          tags,\n362\t          // Falls back to the column default (now()) on first insert.\n363\t          ...(publishedAt ? { publishedAt } : {}),\n364\t        })\n365\t        .onConflictDoUpdate({\n366\t          target: blogPosts.externalId,\n367\t          set: {\n368\t            slug,\n369\t            title,\n370\t            description: description?.trim() || null,\n371\t            contentMarkdown: markdown,\n372\t            contentHtml: html,\n373\t            imageUrl: article.image_url ?? null,\n374\t            imageAlt: article.image_alt ?? title,\n375\t            tags,\n376\t            // Only overwrite publishedAt when the payload supplied a date, so\n377\t            // a re-publish without created_at does not reset ordering state.\n378\t            ...(publishedAt ? { publishedAt } : {}),\n379\t            updatedAt: new Date(),\n380\t          },\n381\t        })\n382\t        .returning({ id: blogPosts.id, slug: blogPosts.slug });\n383\t\n384\t      processed.push({ id: row.id, slug: row.slug });\n385\t\n386\t      revalidatePath(`/en/blog/${row.slug}`);\n387\t    } catch (err) {\n388\t      console.error(\"[add-blog-post] upsert failed\", {\n389\t        externalId: article.id,\n390\t        err,\n391\t      });\n392\t      // A thrown upsert is treated as transient (DB outage, constraint, etc.)\n393\t      // so the delivery is retryable.\n394\t      hadTransientFailure = true;\n395\t      skipped.push({\n396\t        index: i,\n397\t        reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"upsert failed\",\n398\t      });\n399\t    }\n400\t  }\n401\t\n402\t  if (processed.length > 0) {\n403\t    revalidatePath(\"/en/blog\");\n404\t    revalidatePath(\"/sitemap.xml\");\n405\t  }\n406\t\n407\t  // If nothing was persisted because of a TRANSIENT failure (a DB upsert\n408\t  // threw), return a non-2xx so the sender retries instead of treating the\n409\t  // silent failure as success. Validation-only skips are permanent client/data\n410\t  // errors — retrying them never succeeds — so those return 2xx below.\n411\t  if (processed.length === 0 && hadTransientFailure) {\n412\t    return NextResponse.json(\n413\t      {\n414\t        message: \"Webhook failed: no articles were persisted\",\n415\t        processed: 0,\n416\t        skipped: skipped.length,\n417\t        skippedDetails: skipped,\n418\t      },\n419\t      { status: 500 },\n420\t    );\n421\t  }\n422\t\n423\t  return NextResponse.json({\n424\t    message: \"Webhook processed successfully\",\n425\t    processed: processed.length,\n426\t    skipped: skipped.length,\n427\t    articles: processed,\n428\t    ...(skipped.length > 0 ? { skippedDetails: skipped } : {}),\n429\t  });\n430\t}\n431\t"}]},"uuid":"c3868cb4-736e-43da-a5b0-d1c8b353c3cf","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:15.727Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\nimport { revalidatePath } from \"next/cache\";\nimport { createHmac, timingSafeEqual } from \"node:crypto\";\nimport { and, eq, ne } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { timingSafeEqualSecret } from \"@/lib/security/timing-safe-secret\";\nimport { env } from \"@/src/env/server.mjs\";\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { blogPosts } from \"@/src/db/schema/blog-posts\";\n\n/**\n * Outrank \"publish_articles\" webhook.\n *\n * Auth (issue #842): Outrank itself can only authenticate with a static\n * `Authorization: Bearer <OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN>` — its webhook spec has no\n * request signing, so it never sends a signature header. The bearer token is\n * therefore the operative, permanent auth and must be treated as a long-lived\n * shared secret: keep it secret and rotate it if it may have leaked.\n *\n * The optional HMAC path (OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET) only engages when a\n * signing-capable proxy sits in front of this endpoint and sends a signature\n * header. When that secret is unset (the expected state for direct Outrank\n * deliveries) the bearer token is the sole auth — the HMAC branch is then\n * intentionally inert, not dead code, and exists so an integrity-binding proxy\n * can be added later without a code change.\n *\n * Upserts each article into blog_posts (keyed on external_id) and revalidates\n * the blog routes so new posts appear immediately.\n */\n\nexport const runtime = \"nodejs\";\nexport const dynamic = \"force-dynamic\";\n\n// Reject oversized payloads before buffering them into memory. Outrank batches\n// are small; a few MB is generous headroom for a legitimate publish.\nconst MAX_BODY_BYTES = 5 * 1024 * 1024; // 5 MB\n// Cap the number of articles processed in a single delivery so one request\n// cannot tie up the function for an unbounded number of serial DB round-trips.\nconst MAX_ARTICLES = 100;\n\ntype OutrankArticle = {\n  id?: string;\n  title?: string;\n  content_markdown?: string;\n  content_html?: string;\n  meta_description?: string;\n  description?: string;\n  created_at?: string;\n  published_at?: string;\n  image_url?: string;\n  image_alt?: string;\n  slug?: string;\n  tags?: string[];\n  url?: string;\n};\n\ntype OutrankPayload = {\n  event_type?: string;\n  timestamp?: string;\n  data?: { articles?: OutrankArticle[] };\n};\n\n/**\n * Verify a per-request HMAC-SHA256 signature over the raw body. Returns false\n * when no signing secret is configured (so the caller can fall back to the\n * static bearer token).\n */\nfunction isSignatureValid(rawBody: string, header: string | null): boolean {\n  const secret = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET;\n  if (!secret || !header) return false;\n\n  // Accept either a bare hex digest or a `sha256=<hex>` prefixed value.\n  const provided = header.startsWith(\"sha256=\")\n    ? header.slice(\"sha256=\".length).trim()\n    : header.trim();\n\n  const expected = createHmac(\"sha256\", secret).update(rawBody).digest(\"hex\");\n\n  const encoder = new TextEncoder();\n  const providedBuf = encoder.encode(provided.toLowerCase());\n  const expectedBuf = encoder.encode(expected);\n  if (providedBuf.length !== expectedBuf.length) return false;\n  return timingSafeEqual(providedBuf, expectedBuf);\n}\n\nfunction isBearerAuthorized(req: NextRequest): boolean {\n  const expected = env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN;\n  if (!expected) return false;\n  const header = req.headers.get(\"authorization\");\n  if (!header || !header.startsWith(\"Bearer \")) return false;\n  return timingSafeEqualSecret(header.slice(\"Bearer \".length).trim(), expected);\n}\n\nfunction slugify(input: string): string {\n  return input\n    .toString()\n    .toLowerCase()\n    .normalize(\"NFKD\")\n    .replace(/[̀-ͯ]/g, \"\")\n    .replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g, \"-\")\n    .replace(/^-+|-+$/g, \"\")\n    .slice(0, 120);\n}\n\nconst LOCALE = \"en\";\n\n/**\n * Return true when `slug` is free within the locale, i.e. not already claimed\n * by a DIFFERENT external article. (A row owned by `externalId` itself is fine\n * — the upsert resolves that on the externalId conflict target.)\n */\nasync function isSlugFree(slug: string, externalId: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  const existing = await db\n    .select({ id: blogPosts.id })\n    .from(blogPosts)\n    .where(\n      and(\n        eq(blogPosts.locale, LOCALE),\n        eq(blogPosts.slug, slug),\n        ne(blogPosts.externalId, externalId),\n      ),\n    )\n    .limit(1);\n  return existing.length === 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * Resolve a slug that is unique within the locale. If the desired slug is\n * already taken by a DIFFERENT external article, append a suffix derived from\n * the external id so the new article still persists instead of throwing a\n * (locale, slug) unique violation. The first candidate is verified too — and if\n * it is also taken (e.g. duplicate-title articles whose external ids share the\n * leading 8 alphanumerics) we widen the suffix and finally fall back to the\n * full external id, which is globally unique, so a free slug is guaranteed.\n */\nasync function resolveUniqueSlug(\n  desired: string,\n  externalId: string,\n): Promise<string> {\n  if (await isSlugFree(desired, externalId)) return desired;\n\n  const sanitizedId = externalId.replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/gi, \"\").toLowerCase();\n\n  // Try progressively longer slices of the external id, then the full id, so\n  // articles that collide on a short shared prefix still resolve to distinct\n  // slugs. Each candidate is verified before use.\n  const suffixLengths = [8, 16, sanitizedId.length];\n  for (const len of suffixLengths) {\n    const suffix = sanitizedId.slice(0, len);\n    if (!suffix) continue;\n    const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n    const candidate = `${base}-${suffix}`;\n    if (await isSlugFree(candidate, externalId)) return candidate;\n  }\n\n  // Last resort: a timestamp keeps the slug unique even when the external id\n  // alone somehow still collides, so the article persists rather than being\n  // dropped on a unique violation.\n  const suffix = `${sanitizedId || \"post\"}-${Date.now().toString(36)}`;\n  const base = desired.slice(0, 120 - (suffix.length + 1));\n  return `${base}-${suffix}`;\n}\n\nfunction escapeHtml(value: string): string {\n  return value\n    .replace(/&/g, \"&amp;\")\n    .replace(/</g, \"&lt;\")\n    .replace(/>/g, \"&gt;\")\n    .replace(/\"/g, \"&quot;\")\n    .replace(/'/g, \"&#39;\");\n}\n\nfunction markdownToHtml(markdown: string): string {\n  return markdown\n    .trim()\n    .split(/\\n{2,}/)\n    .map((block) => {\n      const text = escapeHtml(block.trim());\n      if (!text) return \"\";\n\n      const heading = text.match(/^(#{1,3})\\s+(.+)$/);\n      if (heading) {\n        const level = heading[1].length;\n        return `<h${level}>${heading[2]}</h${level}>`;\n      }\n\n      return `<p>${text.replace(/\\n/g, \"<br />\")}</p>`;\n    })\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .join(\"\\n\");\n}\n\nfunction parsePublishedAt(article: OutrankArticle): {\n  publishedAt?: Date;\n  error?: string;\n} {\n  const candidate = article.published_at ?? article.created_at;\n  if (!candidate) return {};\n\n  const parsed = new Date(candidate);\n  if (Number.isNaN(parsed.getTime())) {\n    const field = article.published_at ? \"published_at\" : \"created_at\";\n    return { error: `invalid ${field}: ${candidate}` };\n  }\n\n  return { publishedAt: parsed };\n}\n\nfunction normalizeTags(tags: unknown): string[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(tags)) return [];\n\n  return tags\n    .filter((tag): tag is string => typeof tag === \"string\")\n    .map((tag) => tag.trim())\n    .filter(Boolean)\n    .slice(0, 12);\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  const contentLength = Number(req.headers.get(\"content-length\"));\n  if (Number.isFinite(contentLength) && contentLength > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Payload too large\" }, { status: 413 });\n  }\n\n  // Signature verification needs the raw bytes, so we can only HMAC-check after\n  // buffering the body. The bearer token, by contrast, is independent of the\n  // payload — so when HMAC signing is disabled (the normal direct-Outrank\n  // setup) reject a bad bearer token BEFORE allocating the body, instead of\n  // buffering an unsigned, unauthenticated request.\n  const signature =\n    req.headers.get(\"x-outrank-signature\") ??\n    req.headers.get(\"outrank-signature\");\n  const signingEnabled = Boolean(env.OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_SIGNING_SECRET);\n  if (!signingEnabled && !isBearerAuthorized(req)) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n      { status: 401 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Read the raw body once so we can both size-check it and verify an HMAC\n  // signature over the exact bytes.\n  let rawBody: string;\n  try {\n    rawBody = await req.text();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid body\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  if (Buffer.byteLength(rawBody, \"utf8\") > MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Payload too large\" }, { status: 413 });\n  }\n\n  // When signing is enabled, prefer HMAC signature verification but still fall\n  // back to the static bearer token for callers that have not migrated to\n  // signing. (When signing is disabled the bearer token was already verified\n  // above before the body was buffered.)\n  if (signingEnabled) {\n    const authorized =\n      isSignatureValid(rawBody, signature) || isBearerAuthorized(req);\n    if (!authorized) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { error: \"Invalid access token\" },\n        { status: 401 },\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  let payload: OutrankPayload;\n  try {\n    payload = JSON.parse(rawBody) as OutrankPayload;\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid JSON\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  if (payload.event_type !== \"publish_articles\") {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: `Unsupported event_type: ${payload.event_type}` },\n      { status: 400 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const articles = payload.data?.articles;\n  if (!Array.isArray(articles) || articles.length === 0) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"No articles in payload\" },\n      { status: 400 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  if (articles.length > MAX_ARTICLES) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: `Too many articles: ${articles.length} (max ${MAX_ARTICLES})`,\n      },\n      { status: 413 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  const processed: Array<{ id: string; slug: string }> = [];\n  const skipped: Array<{ index: number; reason: string }> = [];\n  // Track whether any skip was a TRANSIENT failure (e.g. a DB upsert error)\n  // rather than a permanent validation error. Only transient failures justify\n  // a non-2xx that asks the sender to retry; a payload whose articles are all\n  // permanently invalid must not be retried forever.\n  let hadTransientFailure = false;\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < articles.length; i++) {\n    const article = articles[i];\n    const markdown = article?.content_markdown?.trim() ?? \"\";\n    const html = article?.content_html?.trim() || markdownToHtml(markdown);\n    const description = article?.meta_description ?? article?.description;\n\n    if (!article?.id || !article.title?.trim() || (!markdown && !html)) {\n      skipped.push({\n        index: i,\n        reason: \"missing id/title/content\",\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const title = article.title.trim();\n    const slugSource = article.slug?.trim() || title;\n    const baseSlug = slugify(slugSource);\n    if (!baseSlug) {\n      skipped.push({ index: i, reason: \"slug resolved to empty string\" });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Validate published_at/created_at before either reaches the driver: an Invalid Date\n    // makes node-postgres throw RangeError on serialization, which would\n    // otherwise be swallowed as a silent skip.\n    const { publishedAt, error: publishedAtError } = parsePublishedAt(article);\n    if (publishedAtError) {\n      skipped.push({\n        index: i,\n        reason: publishedAtError,\n      });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    const tags = normalizeTags(article.tags);\n\n    try {\n      // Avoid a (locale, slug) unique violation when a different article has\n      // already claimed this slug.\n      const slug = await resolveUniqueSlug(baseSlug, article.id);\n\n      const [row] = await db\n        .insert(blogPosts)\n        .values({\n          externalId: article.id,\n          source: \"outrank\",\n          locale: LOCALE,\n          slug,\n          title,\n          description: description?.trim() || null,\n          contentMarkdown: markdown,\n          contentHtml: html,\n          imageUrl: article.image_url ?? null,\n          imageAlt: article.image_alt ?? title,\n          tags,\n          // Falls back to the column default (now()) on first insert.\n          ...(publishedAt ? { publishedAt } : {}),\n        })\n        .onConflictDoUpdate({\n          target: blogPosts.externalId,\n          set: {\n            slug,\n            title,\n            description: description?.trim() || null,\n            contentMarkdown: markdown,\n            contentHtml: html,\n            imageUrl: article.image_url ?? null,\n            imageAlt: article.image_alt ?? title,\n            tags,\n            // Only overwrite publishedAt when the payload supplied a date, so\n            // a re-publish without created_at does not reset ordering state.\n            ...(publishedAt ? { publishedAt } : {}),\n            updatedAt: new Date(),\n          },\n        })\n        .returning({ id: blogPosts.id, slug: blogPosts.slug });\n\n      processed.push({ id: row.id, slug: row.slug });\n\n      revalidatePath(`/en/blog/${row.slug}`);\n    } catch (err) {\n      console.error(\"[add-blog-post] upsert failed\", {\n        externalId: article.id,\n        err,\n      });\n      // A thrown upsert is treated as transient (DB outage, constraint, etc.)\n      // so the delivery is retryable.\n      hadTransientFailure = true;\n      skipped.push({\n        index: i,\n        reason: err instanceof Error ? err.message : \"upsert failed\",\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (processed.length > 0) {\n    revalidatePath(\"/en/blog\");\n    revalidatePath(\"/sitemap.xml\");\n  }\n\n  // If nothing was persisted because of a TRANSIENT failure (a DB upsert\n  // threw), return a non-2xx so the sender retries instead of treating the\n  // silent failure as success. Validation-only skips are permanent client/data\n  // errors — retrying them never succeeds — so those return 2xx below.\n  if (processed.length === 0 && hadTransientFailure) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        message: \"Webhook failed: no articles were persisted\",\n        processed: 0,\n        skipped: skipped.length,\n        skippedDetails: skipped,\n      },\n      { status: 500 },\n    );\n  }\n\n  return NextResponse.json({\n    message: \"Webhook processed successfully\",\n    processed: processed.length,\n    skipped: skipped.length,\n    articles: processed,\n    ...(skipped.length > 0 ? { skippedDetails: skipped } : {}),\n  });\n}\n","numLines":431,"startLine":1,"totalLines":431}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a46db449-5de5-475b-b9c2-60fdb5eb8d65","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

[],\n43\t    slug: row.slug,\n44\t  };\n45\t\n46\t  return {\n47\t    locale: row.locale,\n48\t    slug: normalizeSlug(row.slug),\n49\t    frontMatter,\n50\t    content: row.contentMarkdown,\n51\t    html: row.contentHtml,\n52\t  };\n53\t}\n54\t\n55\tasync function readAllPosts(): Promise<BlogPost[]> {\n56\t  try {\n57\t    const rows = await db\n58\t      .select()\n59\t      .from(blogPosts)\n60\t      .orderBy(desc(blogPosts.publishedAt));\n61\t    return rows\n62\t      .filter((row) => row.locale === BLOG_LOCALE)\n63\t      .map(rowToPost);\n64\t  } catch (err) {\n65\t    console.error(\"[blog] failed to load posts from DB:\", err);\n66\t    return [];\n67\t  }\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\texport async function getBlogPosts(): Promise<BlogPostSummary[]> {\n71\t  const posts = await readAllPosts();\n72\t  return posts.map((post) => ({\n73\t    locale: post.locale,\n74\t    slug: post.slug,\n75\t    frontMatter: post.frontMatter,\n76\t  }));\n77\t}\n78\t\n79\texport async function getBlogPost(slug: string): Promise<BlogPost | null> {\n80\t  const normalizedSlug = normalizeSlug(slug);\n81\t  const posts = await readAllPosts();\n82\t  return posts.find((item) => item.slug === normalizedSlug) ?? \"\",\n    date: row.publishedAt.toISOString(),\n    image: row.imageUrl ?? undefined,\n    imageAlt: row.imageAlt ?? undefined,\n    tags: row.tags ?? 'image/gif';\n  }\n\n  if (\n    bytes.length >= 12\n    && bytes[0] === 0x52\n    && bytes[1] === 0x49\n    && bytes[2] === 0x46\n    && bytes[3] === 0x46\n    && bytes[8] === 0x57\n    && bytes[9] === 0x45\n    && bytes[10] === 0x42\n    && bytes[11] === 0x50\n  ) {\n    return 'image/webp';\n  }\n\n  return null;\n}\n\nexport function validateAssistantContentLength(contentLengthHeader: string | undefined):\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response } {\n  if (!contentLengthHeader) {\n    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Missing Content-Length', 'Content-Length header is required') };\n  }\n\n  const contentLength = Number.parseInt(contentLengthHeader, 10);\n  if (!Number.isFinite(contentLength) || contentLength <= 0) {\n    return { ok: false, response: errorResponse(400, 'Invalid Content-Length', 'Content-Length must be a positive integer') };\n  }\n\n  if (contentLength > MAX_ASSISTANT_BODY_BYTES) {\n    return {\n      ok: false,\n      response: errorResponse(413, 'Request too large',\n        `Request body must be ${Math.round(MAX_ASSISTANT_BODY_BYTES / (1024 * 1024))} MB or smaller`,\n        { max_size_bytes: MAX_ASSISTANT_BODY_BYTES, content_length: contentLength }),\n    };\n  }\n\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\n/**\n * Estimate the decoded byte length of a base64 string without allocating a\n * buffer. Each 4 base64 chars decode to 3 bytes; trailing `=` padding reduces\n * the final group. Good enough to enforce a size cap before forwarding.\n */\nfunction estimateBase64DecodedBytes(base64: string): number {\n  const len = base64.length;\n  if (len === 0) return 0;\n  let padding = 0;\n  if (base64.endsWith('==')) padding = 2;\n  else if (base64.endsWith('=')) padding = 1;\n  return Math.floor((len * 3) / 4) - padding;\n}\n\n/**\n * Convert the client's OpenAI-format messages to Anthropic Messages API format.\n * - Extracts the system message (returned separately for Anthropic's `system` param)\n * - Converts image_url content blocks to Anthropic image blocks\n */\nexport function convertMessages(\n  clientMessages: unknown[],\n  imageBase64: string | null,\n  imageMediaType = DEFAULT_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPE\n): { systemPrompt: string; messages: AnthropicMessage[]; imageCount: number } {\n  let systemPrompt = '';\n  const messages: AnthropicMessage[] = [];\n  // Cap total images (multipart + inline) forwarded to Anthropic so a client\n  // cannot drive unbounded vision spend by embedding many image blocks.\n  let imageCount = 0;\n\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    if (!msg || typeof msg !== 'object') continue;\n    const m = msg as Record<string, unknown>;\n    const role = m.role as string;\n\n    if (role === 'system') {\n      systemPrompt = typeof m.content === 'string' ? m.content : '';\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    if (role !== 'user' && role !== 'assistant') continue;\n\n    // Handle string content\n    if (typeof m.content === 'string') {\n      messages.push({ role, content: m.content });\n      continue;\n    }\n\n    // Handle array content (multimodal — image_url + text)\n    if (Array.isArray(m.content)) {\n      const blocks: AnthropicContentBlock[] = [];\n\n      for (const part of m.content) {\n        if (!part || typeof part !== 'object') continue;\n        const p = part as Record<string, unknown>;\n\n        if (p.type === 'text' && typeof p.text === 'string') {\n          blocks.push({ type: 'text', text: p.text });\n        }\n\n        if (p.type === 'image_url') {\n          // Drop any images beyond the per-request cap instead of forwarding\n          // them to Anthropic (unbounded vision cost).\n          if (imageCount >= ASSISTANT_MAX_IMAGES) continue;\n\n          // The client embeds base64 inline as data:image/jpeg;base64,...\n          // But for HyperWhisper Cloud, the image comes as a separate multipart file\n          // We use the multipart image if available, otherwise parse inline\n          let base64Data = imageBase64;\n          let mediaType = normalizeImageMediaType(imageMediaType) || DEFAULT_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPE;\n\n          if (!base64Data && p.image_url && typeof p.image_url === 'object') {\n            const urlObj = p.image_url as Record<string, unknown>;\n            const url = urlObj.url as string;\n            if (url?.startsWith('data:')) {\n              const match = url.match(/^data:(image\\/\\w+);base64,(.+)$/);\n              if (match) {\n                const parsedMediaType = normalizeImageMediaType(match[1]);\n                // Reject oversized inline images. The multipart path is already\n                // bounded by the request body size gate; inline base64 is not.\n                if (parsedMediaType && estimateBase64DecodedBytes(match[2]) <= ASSISTANT_MAX_INLINE_IMAGE_BYTES) {\n                  mediaType = parsedMediaType;\n                  base64Data = match[2];\n                }\n              }\n            }\n          }\n\n          if (base64Data) {\n            blocks.push({\n              type: 'image',\n              source: {\n                type: 'base64',\n                media_type: mediaType,\n                data: base64Data,\n              },\n            });\n            imageCount += 1;\n          }\n        }\n      }\n\n      if (blocks.length > 0) {\n        messages.push({ role, content: blocks });\n      }\n      continue;\n    }\n  }\n\n  return { systemPrompt, messages, imageCount };\n}\n\n/**\n * Count the inline `image_url` content blocks in client messages, capped at\n * `ASSISTANT_MAX_IMAGES`. Used to scale the upfront credit estimate so a client\n * cannot pass a flat pre-check while forwarding multiple billable images.\n */\nexport function countInlineImages(clientMessages: unknown[]): number {\n  let count = 0;\n  for (const msg of clientMessages) {\n    if (!msg || typeof msg !== 'object') continue;\n    const content = (msg as Record<string, unknown>).content;\n    if (!Array.isArray(content)) continue;\n    for (const part of content) {\n      if (!part || typeof part !== 'object') continue;\n      if ((part as Record<string, unknown>).type === 'image_url') {\n        count += 1;\n        if (count >= ASSISTANT_MAX_IMAGES) return count;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return count;\n}\n\nexport async function assistantRoute(c: Context) {\n  const requestId = generateRequestId();\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n\n  // Size gate BEFORE parsing — `formData()` buffers the entire body into RAM,\n  // so an unauthenticated oversized upload would OOM the machine before auth\n  // runs (same pattern as /transcribe). Content-Length is required so the cap\n  // can't be bypassed with chunked transfer encoding.\n  const sizeCheck = validateAssistantContentLength(c.req.header('Content-Length'));\n  if (!sizeCheck.ok) {\n    return sizeCheck.response;\n  }\n\n  // Parse multipart form data\n  let formData: FormData;\n  try {\n    formData = await c.req.formData();\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid request', 'Request must be multipart/form-data');\n  }\n\n  // `account_key` is the canonical field; `license_key` is the legacy alias that\n  // installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const licenseKey = (formData.get('account_key') ||\n    formData.get('license_key')) as string | null;\n  const messagesRaw = formData.get('messages');\n  const promptOverride = formData.get('prompt') as string | null;\n  const imageFile = formData.get('image') as File | null;\n\n  // Validate messages. A malformed multipart request can send `messages` as a\n  // file part, in which case formData.get returns a File; reject any non-string\n  // before measuring/parsing so it surfaces as a 400 rather than a 500.\n  if (typeof messagesRaw !== 'string' || !messagesRaw) {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Missing field', 'Request must include \"messages\" field');\n  }\n\n  // Cap the serialized messages payload. Inline base64 images live inside this\n  // JSON; without a cap a client could embed many MBs of image data and bypass\n  // the flat credit pre-check, driving unbounded Anthropic vision spend. Sized\n  // to admit ASSISTANT_MAX_IMAGES inline images at ASSISTANT_MAX_INLINE_IMAGE_BYTES\n  // (base64 ~4/3 expansion) plus text headroom.\n  if (Buffer.byteLength(messagesRaw, 'utf8') > ASSISTANT_MAX_MESSAGES_BYTES) {\n    return errorResponse(413, 'Messages too large',\n      `The \"messages\" payload must be ${Math.round(ASSISTANT_MAX_MESSAGES_BYTES / (1024 * 1024))} MB or smaller.`);\n  }\n\n  let clientMessages: unknown[];\n  try {\n    clientMessages = JSON.parse(messagesRaw);\n    if (!Array.isArray(clientMessages)) throw new Error('not an array');\n  } catch {\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Invalid messages', 'Messages must be a valid JSON array');\n  }\n\n  // Auth — Cloud is licensed-only; a valid license key is required.\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({ licenseKey: licenseKey || undefined });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n\n  // Credit check — scale the estimate by the number of images that will be\n  // forwarded so a low-balance client cannot pass a flat pre-check and then\n  // burn vision spend on multiple images.\n  const estimatedImageCount = Math.max(1, countInlineImages(clientMessages));\n  const estimatedCredits = ESTIMATED_ASSISTANT_CREDITS * estimatedImageCount;\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, estimatedCredits, clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n\n  // Read image as base64\n  let imageBase64: string | null = null;\n  let imageMediaType = DEFAULT_IMAGE_MEDIA_TYPE;\n  if (imageFile) {\n    // Hard size cap BEFORE allocating the ArrayBuffer. Reading the file and\n    // base64-encoding it (~1.33x expansion) on an unbounded upload can exhaust\n    // the Bun process and OOM the machine, so reject oversized images upfront.\n    if (imageFile.size > MAX_ASSISTANT_IMAGE_BYTES) {\n      return imageTooLargeResponse(imageFile.size, MAX_ASSISTANT_IMAGE_BYTES);\n    }\n    const imageBuffer = await imageFile.arrayBuffer();\n    const imageBytes = new Uint8Array(imageBuffer);\n    const declaredMediaType = normalizeImageMediaType(imageFile.type);\n    const detectedMediaType = detectImageMediaType(imageBytes);\n    imageMediaType = detectedMediaType || declaredMediaType || '';\n    if (!imageMediaType) {\n      return errorResponse(400, 'Unsupported image type', 'Image must be JPEG, PNG, GIF, or WebP');\n    }\n    imageBase64 = Buffer.from(imageBytes).toString('base64');\n  }\n\n  // Convert messages to Anthropic format\n  const { systemPrompt, messages } = convertMessages(clientMessages, imageBase64, imageMediaType);\n\n  // Use the prompt override if provided (takes precedence over system message in conversation)\n  const finalSystemPrompt = promptOverride || systemPrompt || 'You are a helpful screen-aware assistant.';\n\n  console.log(`[${requestId}] Assistant request: ${messages.length} messages, image=${!!imageBase64}, ip=${clientIP}`);\n\n  // Stream the response\n  const { stream, costPromise } = streamAnthropicChat(finalSystemPrompt, messages, requestId);\n\n  // Deduct credits after stream completes (fire-and-forget)\n  costPromise.then((costUsd) => {\n    if (costUsd > 0) {\n      deductCredits(\n        authResult.value,\n        costUsd,\n        {\n          assistant_cost_usd: costUsd,\n          message_count: messages.length,\n          has_image: !!imageBase64,\n          endpoint: '/assistant',\n          llm_provider: 'anthropic',\n        },\n        clientIP\n      ).catch(console.error);\n    }\n  }).catch(console.error);\n\n  return new Response(stream, {\n    headers: {\n      ...CORS_HEADERS,\n      'Content-Type': 'text/event-stream',\n      'Cache-Control': 'no-cache',\n      'Connection': 'keep-alive',\n      'X-Accel-Buffering': 'no',\n      'X-Request-ID': requestId,\n    },\n  });\n}\n","numLines":378,"startLine":1,"totalLines":378}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"2c45fcd5-848a-47fb-a7a6-9ab40ec43183","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Adds ~50-80ms vs ~6s of failure.\n  // Verified blocked regions (2026-06-07): nrt (JP), bom (IN), maa (IN).\n  //\n  // Fly only honours `fly-replay` for request bodies ≤ 1 MB; larger requests\n  // are silently executed in the original region. For oversized uploads from\n  // a blocked region we skip the replay header and let the chain fall back\n  // to the next provider instead of letting ElevenLabs return its HTML 200.\n  let elevenlabsGeoBlocked = false;\n  if (provider === 'elevenlabs' && ELEVENLABS_BLOCKED_FLY_REGIONS.has(process.env.FLY_REGION || '')) {\n    if (contentLength <= FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES) {\n      logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay', {\n        flyRequestId,\n        provider,\n        fromRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n        toRegion: ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION,\n        reason: 'elevenlabs_geo_block',\n      });\n      c.header('fly-replay', `region=${ELEVENLABS_REPLAY_REGION}`);\n      return c.body(null, 200);\n    }\n\n    elevenlabsGeoBlocked = true;\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.fly_replay_skipped_oversized', {\n      flyRequestId,\n      provider,\n      flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION,\n      contentLength,\n      replayMaxBytes: FLY_REPLAY_MAX_BODY_BYTES,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const proxyOverheadMs = flyProxyOverheadMs(c.req.header('Fly-Request-Start'));\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_start', {\n    flyRequestId,\n    flyRegion: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    flyMachineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID,\n    proxyOverheadMs,\n    provider,\n    model: model || 'default',\n    domain: domain || 'none',\n    contentType,\n    contentLength,\n    language: language || 'auto',\n    hasInitialPrompt: Boolean(initialPrompt),\n    mode: mode || 'default',\n  });\n\n  // Auth (query params only) — Cloud is licensed-only; a valid account key is required.\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param name; `license_key` is the legacy alias\n  // that installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const authResult = await validateAuth({\n    licenseKey:\n      rawQuery(c.req.url, 'account_key') ?? rawQuery(c.req.url, 'license_key'),\n  });\n  if (!authResult.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'auth_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: authResult.response.status,\n    });\n    return authResult.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.auth_done');\n\n  // Vocabulary surcharge: AssemblyAI charges a keyterms_prompt add-on (universal-3-pro)\n  // and ElevenLabs a +20% keyterm surcharge (scribe_v2) when an initial_prompt is supplied.\n  // We pass the raw hasInitialPrompt flag through to the reservation so it can reserve the\n  // surcharge for ANY eligible chain member — including ElevenLabs reached via a\n  // Deepgram/Groq/Grok fallback, which still forwards the prompt and bills the surcharge.\n  // estimatedUsdPerMinute scopes the add-on to universal-3-pro / scribe_v2, so passing it\n  // for every request is safe and never under-reserves.\n  const estimatedCredits = estimateCreditsForProviderFallbacks(contentLength, provider, model, medical, initialPrompt, language);\n  const creditCheck = await validateCredits(authResult.value, estimatedCredits, clientIP);\n  if (!creditCheck.ok) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'credits_failed',\n      flyRequestId,\n      status: creditCheck.response.status,\n      estimatedCredits,\n    });\n    return creditCheck.response;\n  }\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.credits_done', { estimatedCredits });\n\n  const uploadStart = performance.now();\n  const audioBuffer = await c.req.arrayBuffer();\n  const uploadMs = Math.round(performance.now() - uploadStart);\n  const uploadBytesPerSec = uploadMs > 0\n    ? Math.round((audioBuffer.byteLength / uploadMs) * 1000)\n    : undefined;\n  logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.buffer_read_done', {\n    audioBytes: audioBuffer.byteLength,\n    uploadMs,\n    uploadBytesPerSec,\n  });\n\n  // The credit check above trusted the declared Content-Length. Reject bodies\n  // that arrive larger than declared so a client can't under-declare to pass\n  // validateCredits cheaply and then stream a bigger payload we'd pay the\n  // provider for (issue ray-amjad/hyperwhisper#263). Honest clients always\n  // send a body that matches Content-Length exactly.\n  if (audioBuffer.byteLength > contentLength) {\n    logEvent(requestId, startTime, 'transcribe.request_rejected', {\n      reason: 'content_length_mismatch',\n      flyRequestId,\n      declaredBytes: contentLength,\n      actualBytes: audioBuffer.byteLength,\n    });\n    return errorResponse(400, 'Content-Length mismatch',\n      `Request body (${audioBuffer.byteLength} bytes) exceeds the declared Content-Length (${contentLength} bytes)`,\n      { requestId, declared_bytes: contentLength, actual_bytes: audioBuffer.byteLength },\n    );\n  }\n\n  let result: TranscriptionResult | undefined;\n  let fallbackFrom: Provider | undefined;\n  let fallbackCount = 0;\n  // The model that actually produced the result. Defaults to the requested\n  // model; on a cross-provider fallback it becomes that sibling's default model.\n  let usedModel = model;\n\n  // When the request landed in a region where ElevenLabs is geo-blocked AND\n  // the payload was too large to fly-replay, drop ElevenLabs from the chain\n  // so we fall through to the next provider instead of failing the chain on\n  // ElevenLabs's HTML-200 geo-block response.\n  const chain = elevenlabsGeoBlocked\n    ? FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider].filter(p => p !== 'elevenlabs')\n    : FALLBACK_CHAINS[provider];\n  let lastError: Error | undefined;\n  let lastInputError: ProviderInputError | undefined;\n  let sawUnavailable = false;\n  // Per-attempt failure breadcrumbs, surfaced on the final outcome log so one\n  // line explains a degraded/failed request (which provider failed, why, how\n  // long it hung) without correlating separate provider-level log events.","numLines":300,"startLine":180,"totalLines":735}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b93b23d8-658a-4523-8428-b496ca69aa6c","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

RFC 3986 query strings have no such rule, and neither\n5\t// platform client form-encodes: a vocabulary term like `C++` sent as\n6\t// `initial_prompt=C%2B%2B` (or even a raw `C++`) must arrive as `C++`, not\n7\t// `C  `. This helper re-parses the query string manually and decodes with\n8\t// `decodeURIComponent`, which leaves `+` alone.\n9\t//\n10\t// Scope: used by the /transcribe route only (see routes/transcribe.ts). Other\n11\t// routes keep `c.req.query()` — their params (keys, ids) never carry `+`.\n12\t\n13\t/**\n14\t * Read a single query parameter from `url`, percent-decoding the value while\n15\t * preserving literal `+`. Returns the FIRST occurrence, `undefined` when the\n16\t * parameter is absent or its value is empty (preserving the callers'\n17\t * `|| undefined` semantics), and the raw (undecoded) value when the value\n18\t * contains a malformed percent-escape.\n19\t */\n20\texport function rawQuery(url: string, name: string): string | undefined {\n21\t  const queryStart = url.indexOf('?');\n22\t  if (queryStart === -1) return undefined;\n23\t\n24\t  // Drop a fragment if present (defensive; clients don't send one).\n25\t  const hashStart = url.indexOf('#', queryStart);\n26\t  const query = url.slice(queryStart + 1, hashStart === -1 ? undefined : hashStart);\n27\t\n28\t  for (const pair of query.split('&')) {\n29\t    const eq = pair.indexOf('=');\n30\t    const rawKey = eq === -1 ? pair : pair.slice(0, eq);\n31\t\n32\t    let key: string;\n33\t    try {\n34\t      key = decodeURIComponent(rawKey);\n35\t    } catch {\n36\t      key = rawKey;\n37\t    }\n38\t    if (key !== name) continue;\n39\t\n40\t    const rawValue = eq === -1 ? '' : pair.slice(eq + 1);\n41\t    let value: string;\n42\t    try {\n43\t      value = decodeURIComponent(rawValue);\n44\t    } catch {\n45\t      // Malformed escape (e.g. a stray `%`): fall back to the raw value rather\n46\t      // than throwing a 500 for one bad byte.\n47\t      value = rawValue;\n48\t    }\n49\t    return value === '' ? undefined : value;\n50\t  }\n51\t\n52\t  return undefined;\n53\t}\n54\t"}]},"uuid":"f0dcb837-8a7f-4933-aacd-3055235ed33a","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:44:49.612Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/lib/query.ts","content":"// Raw query-string parsing that preserves literal `+`.\n//\n// Hono's `c.req.query()` decodes query values with an extra `+` → space step\n// (HTML-form semantics). RFC 3986 query strings have no such rule, and neither\n// platform client form-encodes: a vocabulary term like `C++` sent as\n// `initial_prompt=C%2B%2B` (or even a raw `C++`) must arrive as `C++`, not\n// `C  `. This helper re-parses the query string manually and decodes with\n// `decodeURIComponent`, which leaves `+` alone.\n//\n// Scope: used by the /transcribe route only (see routes/transcribe.ts). Other\n// routes keep `c.req.query()` — their params (keys, ids) never carry `+`.\n\n/**\n * Read a single query parameter from `url`, percent-decoding the value while\n * preserving literal `+`. Returns the FIRST occurrence, `undefined` when the\n * parameter is absent or its value is empty (preserving the callers'\n * `|| undefined` semantics), and the raw (undecoded) value when the value\n * contains a malformed percent-escape.\n */\nexport function rawQuery(url: string, name: string): string | undefined {\n  const queryStart = url.indexOf('?');\n  if (queryStart === -1) return undefined;\n\n  // Drop a fragment if present (defensive; clients don't send one).\n  const hashStart = url.indexOf('#', queryStart);\n  const query = url.slice(queryStart + 1, hashStart === -1 ? undefined : hashStart);\n\n  for (const pair of query.split('&')) {\n    const eq = pair.indexOf('=');\n    const rawKey = eq === -1 ? pair : pair.slice(0, eq);\n\n    let key: string;\n    try {\n      key = decodeURIComponent(rawKey);\n    } catch {\n      key = rawKey;\n    }\n    if (key !== name) continue;\n\n    const rawValue = eq === -1 ? credits: number }> {\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      return { isValid: cached.isValid, credits: cached.credits };\n    }\n  }\n\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/validate`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },\n      body: JSON.stringify({ license_key: licenseKey, include_credits: true }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({})) as { valid?: boolean; credits?: number };\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = readFiniteCredits(data) ?? 0;\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch {\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nasync function getCreditsBalance(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ credits: number; error?: string }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits?license_key=${encodeURIComponent(licenseKey)}`, {\n      method: 'GET',\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({})) as { error?: string };\n      return { credits: 0, error: errorData.error || `HTTP ${response.status}` };\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n    const credits = readFiniteCredits(data);\n\n    if (credits === null) {\n      return { credits: 0, error: 'Invalid credits response' };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid: true,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    return { credits: 0, error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error) };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function usageRoute(c: Context) {\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return errorResponse(403, 'Access denied', 'Your IP has been temporarily blocked due to abuse');\n  }\n\n  // `account_key` is the canonical param; `license_key` is the legacy alias that\n  // installed native apps still send, so we accept either.\n  const licenseKey =\n    c.req.query('account_key') || c.req.query('license_key') || c.req.query('identifier')?.trim() || null;\n  const forceRefresh = c.req.query('force_refresh') === 'true';\n\n  if (licenseKey) {\n    let isValid = false;\n    let credits = 0;\n\n    if (forceRefresh) {\n      const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n      if (cached?.isValid) {\n        const balanceResult = await getCreditsBalance(licenseKey);\n        if (balanceResult.error) {\n          const validation = await validateLicenseAndGetCredits(licenseKey, true);\n          isValid = validation.isValid;\n          credits = validation.credits;\n        } else {\n          isValid = true;\n          credits = balanceResult.credits;\n        }\n      } else {\n        const validation = await validateLicenseAndGetCredits(licenseKey, true);\n        isValid = validation.isValid;\n        credits = validation.credits;\n      }\n    } else {\n      const validation = await validateLicenseAndGetCredits(licenseKey, false);\n      isValid = validation.isValid;\n      credits = validation.credits;\n    }\n\n    if (!isValid) {\n      return errorResponse(401, 'Invalid license key', 'The provided license key is invalid or expired');\n    }\n\n    const normalizedCredits = roundToTenth(credits);\n    const minutesRemaining = Math.floor(normalizedCredits / CREDITS_PER_MINUTE);\n\n    const response = {\n      credits_remaining: normalizedCredits,\n      minutes_remaining: minutesRemaining,\n      credits_per_minute: CREDITS_PER_MINUTE,\n      is_licensed: true,\n      is_trial: false,\n      is_anonymous: false,\n    };\n\n    return jsonResponse(response);\n  }\n\n  return errorResponse(401, 'License required', 'You must provide a valid license_key. UPSTASH REDIS CLIENT\n// Serverless Redis for IP blocking and license caching\n// Works globally with Fly.io's anycast routing\n\nimport { Redis } from '@upstash/redis';\nimport { LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS } from './constants';\n\n// Initialize Redis client (lazy initialization for testing without Redis)\nlet _redis: Redis | null = null;\n\nfunction getRedis(): Redis {\n  if (!_redis) {\n    const url = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_URL;\n    const token = process.env.UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN;\n\n    if (!url || !token) {\n      throw new Error('UPSTASH_REDIS_URL and UPSTASH_REDIS_TOKEN are required');\n    }\n\n    _redis = new Redis({ url, token });\n  }\n  return _redis;\n}\n\n// Export redis getter for lazy initialization\nexport const redis = {\n  get: getRedis,\n};\n\n// ============================================================================\n// IP BLOCKING + DAILY QUOTA (credits-based)\n// ============================================================================\n\nexport async function isIPBlocked(ip: string): Promise<boolean> {\n  try {\n    const blockKey = `ip_blocked:${ip}`;\n    const blocked = await getRedis().get(blockKey);\n    return blocked === 'true';\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// LICENSE CACHE (1 hour TTL for valid + invalid)\n// ============================================================================\n\nexport interface CachedLicense {\n  isValid: boolean;\n  credits: number;\n  cachedAt: string;\n}\n\nexport async function getCachedLicense(licenseKey: string): Promise<CachedLicense | null> {\n  try {\n    const cached = await getRedis().get<CachedLicense>(`license:${licenseKey}`);\n    if (!cached) return null;\n\n    if (typeof cached === 'string') {\n      return JSON.parse(cached) as CachedLicense;\n    }\n\n    return cached;\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error('Failed to get cached license:', error);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function cacheLicense(licenseKey: string, license: CachedLicense): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await getRedis().set(`license:${licenseKey}`, {"parentUuid":"d7b4e2e8-94d0-463a-a095-b13efb69bc80","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016rcLZbvx9SX69ac9edPibs","type":"tool_result","content":"bun.lock\nCLAUDE.md\nDockerfile\nfly.dev.toml\nfly.prod.toml\nfly.staging.toml\nLICENSE\nnode_modules\npackage.json\npnpm-lock.yaml\nREADME.md\nreferences\nscripts\nsrc\ntsconfig.json\n=== index.ts ===\n// HYPERWHISPER FLY.IO TRANSCRIPTION SERVICE\n// Edge-based transcription proxy replacing Cloudflare Workers\n// Eliminates R2 upload path complexity - can buffer larger audio files in memory\n\nimport { Hono } from 'hono';\nimport { cors } from 'hono/cors';\nimport { websocket } from 'hono/bun';\nimport { transcribeRoute } from './routes/transcribe';\nimport { postProcessRoute } from './routes/post-process';\nimport { assistantRoute } from './routes/assistant';\nimport { usageRoute } from './routes/usage';\nimport { wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute } from './routes/ws-streaming-deepgram';\nimport { drainPendingDeductions } from './middleware/credits';\n\nconst app = new Hono();\n\n// CORS — allow the custom STT selection headers so browser callers can set\n// provider/model/domain. Without these in allowHeaders the preflight blocks the\n// POST before it reaches the route (X-STT-* are non-simple request headers).\napp.use('*', cors({\n  origin: '*',\n  allowHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'X-STT-Provider', 'X-STT-Model', 'X-STT-Domain'],\n  allowMethods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS'],\n}));\n\napp.options('*', (c) => c.body(null, 204));\n\n// Health check endpoint - Fly.io uses this for health monitoring\napp.get('/health', (c) => {\n  return c.json({\n    status: 'ok',\n    region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n});\n\n// Warmup endpoint - clients hit this on hotkey-down to pre-establish\n// the TLS/HTTP2 connection before the /transcribe POST.\napp.get('/warmup', (c) => {\n  c.header('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n  return c.body(null, 204);\n});\n\n// Main transcription endpoint\napp.post('/transcribe', transcribeRoute);\n\n// Standalone post-processing endpoint\napp.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute);\n\n// Assistant mode endpoint (vision LLM)\napp.post('/assistant', assistantRoute);\n\n// Usage endpoint\napp.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n\n// WebSocket streaming endpoint\napp.get('/ws/streaming-deepgram', wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute);\n\n// Fallback - match Cloudflare (405, plain text)\napp.notFound((c) => {\n  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n});\n\n// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Log the full error\n// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\napp.onError((err, c) => {\n  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n  return c.json({\n    error: 'Internal server error',\n    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.',\n    error_id: errorId,\n  }, 500);\n});\n\n// Graceful shutdown: Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace\n// period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s). Credit deductions are\n// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n// but the charge is silently dropped.\nconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\nlet shuttingDown = false;\n\nasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (shuttingDown) return;\n  shuttingDown = true;\n\n  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n    signal,\n    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n  if (drained > 0) {\n    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n  }\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); });\nprocess.on('SIGINT', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'); });\n\n// Export for Bun\nexport default {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  fetch: app.fetch,\n  websocket,\n};\n\nconsole.log('machine.boot', {\n  region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n  machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n  allocId: process.env.FLY_ALLOC_ID || undefined,\n  imageRef: process.env.FLY_IMAGE_REF || undefined,\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  bootAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n});\nconsole.log(`HyperWhisper Fly.io service starting on port ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);\nconsole.log(`Region: ${process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local'}`);\nconsole.log(`License API: ${process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || 'https://www.hyperwhisper.com (default)'}`);\nconsole.log('Started server: http://localhost:' + (process.env.PORT || 8080));","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"f0ae3081-9e9c-462e-9069-857823cb16d9","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:02.018Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"bun.lock\nCLAUDE.md\nDockerfile\nfly.dev.toml\nfly.prod.toml\nfly.staging.toml\nLICENSE\nnode_modules\npackage.json\npnpm-lock.yaml\nREADME.md\nreferences\nscripts\nsrc\ntsconfig.json\n=== index.ts ===\n// HYPERWHISPER FLY.IO TRANSCRIPTION SERVICE\n// Edge-based transcription proxy replacing Cloudflare Workers\n// Eliminates R2 upload path complexity - can buffer larger audio files in memory\n\nimport { Hono } from 'hono';\nimport { cors } from 'hono/cors';\nimport { websocket } from 'hono/bun';\nimport { transcribeRoute } from './routes/transcribe';\nimport { postProcessRoute } from './routes/post-process';\nimport { assistantRoute } from './routes/assistant';\nimport { usageRoute } from './routes/usage';\nimport { wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute } from './routes/ws-streaming-deepgram';\nimport { drainPendingDeductions } from './middleware/credits';\n\nconst app = new Hono();\n\n// CORS — allow the custom STT selection headers so browser callers can set\n// provider/model/domain. Without these in allowHeaders the preflight blocks the\n// POST before it reaches the route (X-STT-* are non-simple request headers).\napp.use('*', cors({\n  origin: '*',\n  allowHeaders: ['Content-Type', 'X-STT-Provider', 'X-STT-Model', 'X-STT-Domain'],\n  allowMethods: ['GET', 'POST', 'OPTIONS'],\n}));\n\napp.options('*', (c) => c.body(null, 204));\n\n// Health check endpoint - Fly.io uses this for health monitoring\napp.get('/health', (c) => {\n  return c.json({\n    status: 'ok',\n    region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n    timestamp: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n});\n\n// Warmup endpoint - clients hit this on hotkey-down to pre-establish\n// the TLS/HTTP2 connection before the /transcribe POST.\napp.get('/warmup', (c) => {\n  c.header('Cache-Control', 'no-store');\n  return c.body(null, 204);\n});\n\n// Main transcription endpoint\napp.post('/transcribe', transcribeRoute);\n\n// Standalone post-processing endpoint\napp.post('/post-process', postProcessRoute);\n\n// Assistant mode endpoint (vision LLM)\napp.post('/assistant', assistantRoute);\n\n// Usage endpoint\napp.get('/usage', usageRoute);\n\n// WebSocket streaming endpoint\napp.get('/ws/streaming-deepgram', wsStreamingPreflight, wsStreamingRoute);\n\n// Fallback - match Cloudflare (405, plain text)\napp.notFound((c) => {\n  return c.text('Method not allowed', 405);\n});\n\n// Error handler — never echo raw err.message to the client (it can contain\n// env-var names, upstream provider bodies, request IDs). Log the full error\n// server-side with an error_id the client can quote to support.\napp.onError((err, c) => {\n  const errorId = crypto.randomUUID();\n  console.error(`Unhandled error [${errorId}]:`, err);\n  return c.json({\n    error: 'Internal server error',\n    message: 'An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.',\n    error_id: errorId,\n  }, 500);\n});\n\n// Graceful shutdown: Fly sends SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down with a grace\n// period before SIGKILL (kill_timeout, default 5s). Credit deductions are\n// fired without awaiting after the response is flushed, so drain any that are\n// still in flight before exiting — otherwise the user keeps the transcript\n// but the charge is silently dropped.\nconst SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS = 4_000;\nlet shuttingDown = false;\n\nasync function gracefulShutdown(signal: string): Promise<void> {\n  if (shuttingDown) return;\n  shuttingDown = true;\n\n  console.log('machine.shutdown', {\n    signal,\n    machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n    shutdownAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n  });\n\n  const drained = await drainPendingDeductions(SHUTDOWN_DRAIN_MS);\n  if (drained > 0) {\n    console.log('machine.shutdown_drained_deductions', { count: drained });\n  }\n  process.exit(0);\n}\n\nprocess.on('SIGTERM', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGTERM'); });\nprocess.on('SIGINT', () => { void gracefulShutdown('SIGINT'); });\n\n// Export for Bun\nexport default {\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  fetch: app.fetch,\n  websocket,\n};\n\nconsole.log('machine.boot', {\n  region: process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local',\n  machineId: process.env.FLY_MACHINE_ID || 'local',\n  allocId: process.env.FLY_ALLOC_ID || undefined,\n  imageRef: process.env.FLY_IMAGE_REF || undefined,\n  port: Number(process.env.PORT) || 8080,\n  bootAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n});\nconsole.log(`HyperWhisper Fly.io service starting on port ${process.env.PORT || 8080}`);\nconsole.log(`Region: ${process.env.FLY_REGION || 'local'}`);\nconsole.log(`License API: ${process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || 'https://www.hyperwhisper.com (default)'}`);\nconsole.log('Started server: http://localhost:' + (process.env.PORT || 8080));","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"d7b4e2e8-94d0-463a-a095-b13efb69bc80","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n32\t *\n33\t * POLAR FALLBACK:\n34\t * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n35\t * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n36\t * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n37\t * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n38\t */\n39\t\n40\t/**\n41\t * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n42\t * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n43\t */\n44\tasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n45\t  licenseKeyId: string,\n46\t  deviceId: string,\n47\t  deviceName?: string\n48\t): Promise<void> {\n49\t  try {\n50\t    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n51\t  } catch (error) {\n52\t    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n53\t    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n54\t  }\n55\t}\n56\t\n57\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n58\t  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n59\t  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n60\t  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n61\t  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n62\t  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n63\t\n64\t  if (!success) {\n65\t    return NextResponse.json(\n66\t      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests.
