Please try again later.\" },\n132\t      { status: 500 }\n133\t    );\n134\t  }\n135\t}\n136\t"}]},"uuid":"e46c3e07-05d7-454e-9c86-125dbbe46a3c","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:10.910Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/license/validate/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport {\n  upsertDeviceValidation,\n  getCreditBalance,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { checkLicenseKey } from \"@/src/lib/license-validation\";\nimport { licenseValidateRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"@/server/api/routers/download-ip\";\n\n/**\n * License Validation API\n *\n * Validates license keys against the database.\n * Supports both Polar-issued and Stripe-issued licenses.\n *\n * DEVICE TRACKING:\n * When device_id is provided, tracks the device validation in the\n * device_validations table for fair usage policy monitoring.\n *\n * BACKWARD COMPATIBILITY:\n * - macOS app calls this endpoint with same request/response format\n * - device_id is optional - old apps without it still work\n *\n * EXTENDED RESPONSE (when include_credits=true):\n * - Returns credit balance for CF Workers to cache\n * - Used by HyperWhisper Cloud for usage-based billing\n *\n * CHECKS:\n * 1. License key exists in database\n * 2. Status is \"granted\" (not revoked/disabled)\n *\n * POLAR FALLBACK:\n * If license not found in database, validates against Polar API.\n * If valid via Polar, imports the license to the database with 5000 credits.\n * (Lookup + fallback + status check live in src/lib/license-validation.ts,\n * shared with the legacy /activate endpoint.)\n */\n\n/**\n * Tracks device validation for fair usage monitoring.\n * This is non-blocking - errors are logged but don't fail the validation.\n */\nasync function trackDeviceValidation(\n  licenseKeyId: string,\n  deviceId: string,\n  deviceName?: string\n): Promise<void> {\n  try {\n    await upsertDeviceValidation(licenseKeyId, deviceId, deviceName);\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Log but don't fail validation - tracking is non-critical\n    console.error(\"Device tracking error:\", error);\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  // Rate limit by IP before any DB lookup or Polar fallback. The fallback\n  // issues a live outbound Polar request on every unknown key, so this caps\n  // the amplification an unauthenticated caller can drive.\n  const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(req.headers);\n  const { success } = await licenseValidateRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n  if (!success) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 429 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  let body: unknown;\n  try {\n    body = await req.json();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Invalid request body\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  const { license_key, include_credits, device_id, device_name } =\n    (body ?? {}) as {\n      license_key?: string;\n      include_credits?: boolean;\n      device_id?: string;\n      device_name?: string;\n    };\n\n  if (!license_key) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"License key is required\" },\n      { status: 400 }\n    );\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Lookup in database, with Polar fallback + status check\n    const result = await checkLicenseKey(license_key);\n\n    if (!result.valid) {\n      return NextResponse.json(\n        { valid: false, error: result.error },\n        { status: result.status }\n      );\n    }\n\n    const license = result.license;\n\n    // DEVICE TRACKING: Record device validation for fair usage monitoring\n    if (device_id) {\n      await trackDeviceValidation(license.id, device_id, device_name);\n    }\n\n    // License is valid - return extended info if requested\n    if (include_credits) {\n      const credits = await getCreditBalance(license.userId);\n\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        valid: true,\n        credits,\n        stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId || null,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Basic response for macOS app compatibility\n    return NextResponse.json({ valid: true });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"License validation error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { valid: false, error: \"Failed to validate license. Please try again later.\" },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":136,"startLine":1,"totalLines":136}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c0ddceac-a80e-46a9-9194-f1a68f57e93a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Either is accepted.\n  account_key?: string;\n  license_key?: string;\n}\n\n// Per-provider primary retry count. Fast/cheap providers retry more; pricier or\n// slower ones retry less to bound latency and spend before falling back.\nfunction retriesFor(provider: LLMProvider): number {\n  switch (provider) {\n    case 'anthropic': return 2;\n    case 'cerebras': return 0;\n    case 'grok': return 1;\n    case 'openai': return 1;\n    case 'gemini': return 2;\n    case 'mistral': return 2;\n    case 'groq': return 3;\n  }\n  // Exhaustiveness guard: if a new LLMProvider is added to the union without a\n  // case above, TypeScript flags this assignment.\n  const _exhaustive: never = provider;\n  throw new Error(`Unhandled LLM provider: ${String(_exhaustive)}`);\n}\n\nfunction rawResponseLength(raw: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (typeof raw === 'string') {\n    return raw.length;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    return JSON.stringify(raw).length;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"9a9c8283-7be4-4dc4-8764-48f21fdea8f4","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:12.792Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"hyperwhisper-cloud/fly.dev.toml:24:  memory = '1gb'\r\nhyperwhisper-cloud/fly.dev.toml:26:  memory_mb = 1024\r\nhyperwhisper-cloud/fly.prod.toml:24:  memory = '1gb'\nhyperwhisper-cloud/fly.prod.toml:26:  memory_mb = 1024\n=== MAX_TEXT_LENGTH ===\n// POST-PROCESS ROUTE\n// POST /post-process - Either is accepted.\n  account_key?: string;\n  license_key?: string;\n}\n\n// Per-provider primary retry count. Fast/cheap providers retry more; pricier or\n// slower ones retry less to bound latency and spend before falling back.\nfunction retriesFor(provider: LLMProvider): number {\n  switch (provider) {\n    case 'anthropic': return 2;\n    case 'cerebras': return 0;\n    case 'grok': return 1;\n    case 'openai': return 1;\n    case 'gemini': return 2;\n    case 'mistral': return 2;\n    case 'groq': return 3;\n  }\n  // Exhaustiveness guard: if a new LLMProvider is added to the union without a\n  // case above, TypeScript flags this assignment.\n  const _exhaustive: never = provider;\n  throw new Error(`Unhandled LLM provider: ${String(_exhaustive)}`);\n}\n\nfunction rawResponseLength(raw: unknown): number | undefined {\n  if (typeof raw === 'string') {\n    return raw.length;\n  }\n\n  try {\n    return JSON.stringify(raw).length;\n  } catch {\n    return undefined;","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"fc1c1332-6d69-4de9-9b99-11d8846a68b3","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{"parentUuid":"06fc6435-63bb-4229-919c-577e99ff5b7f","isSidechain":true,"agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","message":{"model":"claude-opus-5","id":"msg_011CdWHtdUo3GEooNussrRjN","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_018SfyezJDDmws5E4biEraeo","name":"Bash","input":{"command":"cd /Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public && for f in nextjs/lib/templates/*.ts; do echo \"===== $f\"; cat \"$f\"; done"},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":"tool_use","stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1838,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2693,"cache_read_input_tokens":90118,"output_tokens":472,"server_tool_use":{"web_search_requests":0,"web_fetch_requests":0},"service_tier":"standard","cache_creation":{"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2693},"inference_geo":"not_available","iterations":[{"input_tokens":1838,"output_tokens":472,"cache_read_input_tokens":90118,"cache_creation_input_tokens":2693,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":2693,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"type":"message"}],"speed":"standard"},"diagnostics":null},"requestId":"req_011CdWHtcBfS4XQSmfFVsss4","attributionAgent":"general-purpose","type":"assistant","uuid":"90b2cfbe-70d7-48b4-9fe1-55ebb388555c","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:45:20.322Z","effort":"high","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"90b2cfbe-70d7-48b4-9fe1-55ebb388555c","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_018SfyezJDDmws5E4biEraeo","type":"tool_result","content":"===== nextjs/lib/templates/credit-mint-email.ts\nimport { escapeHtml } from \"./escape-html\";\n\nexport interface CreditMintEmailData {\n  customerName: string;\n  customerEmail: string;\n  licenseKey: string;\n  /** Credits granted by this purchase (also the starting balance). */\n  creditAmount: number;\n  productName: string;\n  supportEmail: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Email for the buy-credits-mints-a-key flow: a guest bought credits with no\n * existing key, so we minted one. Delivers the new key AND the starting credit\n * balance (the two are the same on a fresh key).\n */\nexport const creditMintEmailHtml = (data: CreditMintEmailData) => {\n  const { licenseKey, productName, supportEmail } = data;\n  // Escape attacker-controllable values (e.g. Stripe customer_details.name)\n  // before embedding them in the email HTML to prevent markup injection.\n  const customerName = escapeHtml(data.customerName);\n  const credits = data.creditAmount.toLocaleString();\n\n  return `\n<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n    <title>Your ${productName} key and credits</title>\n</head>\n<body style=\"font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 1.6; color: #333; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 20px; background-color: #ffffff;\">\n    <div style=\"background-color: #f9fafb; border-radius: 8px; padding: 32px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <h1 style=\"color: #2563eb; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 24px;\">Your ${productName} key is ready</h1>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 8px; color: #4b5563;\">Hi ${customerName},</p>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 16px; color: #4b5563;\">Thanks for your purchase! Your Account Key unlocks your credit wallet — your credits are loaded and ready to use.</p>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #eff6ff; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; margin: 24px 0; border-left: 4px solid #2563eb;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #1e40af; font-weight: 600;\">Your Account Key</p>\n            <div style=\"margin-top: 12px; background-color: #dbeafe; padding: 12px; border-radius: 4px;\">\n                <p style=\"margin: 0; font-family: 'Courier New', monospace; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; color: #1e40af; letter-spacing: 2px; word-break: break-all; text-align: center;\">${licenseKey}</p>\n            </div>\n            <p style=\"margin: 12px 0 0 0; color: #1e40af; font-size: 14px;\">Starting balance: <strong>${credits} credits</strong></p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #f3f4f6; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; margin: 24px 0;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0 0 12px 0; color: #374151; font-weight: 600; font-size: 18px;\">Quick Start</p>\n            <ol style=\"margin: 0; padding-left: 20px; color: #4b5563;\">\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Open ${productName}</li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Go to Settings → License</li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Paste your Account Key: <strong>${licenseKey}</strong></li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Start using HyperWhisper Cloud</li>\n            </ol>\n        </div>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #fffbeb; border-radius: 8px; padding: 16px; margin-top: 24px; border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #92400e; font-size: 14px;\">Credits are valid for 12 months from purchase. You can top up anytime from your <a href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/user/dashboard\" style=\"color: #92400e;\">dashboard</a>.</p>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <p style=\"color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <strong>Need help?</strong> Email us at <a href=\"mailto:${supportEmail}\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">${supportEmail}</a> or visit our <a href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/user\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">customer portal</a>.\n    </p>\n\n    <hr style=\"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e5e7eb; margin: 24px 0;\">\n    <p style=\"color: #9ca3af; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;\">This email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because a purchase was made for ${productName}.</p>\n    <p style=\"font-size: 11px; color: #9ca3af; text-align: center; margin: 24px 0 0 0;\">\n        Ray Amjad LTD<br />\n        Lytchett House, 13 Freeland Park, Wareham Road, Poole, Dorset, BH16 6FA<br />\n        <a href=\"mailto:hello@hyperwhisper.com\" style=\"color: #9ca3af;\">hello@hyperwhisper.com</a>\n    </p>\n</body>\n</html>\n`;\n};\n\nexport const creditMintEmailText = (data: CreditMintEmailData) => {\n  const { customerName, licenseKey, productName, supportEmail } = data;\n  const credits = data.creditAmount.toLocaleString();\n\n  return `\nYour ${productName} key is ready\n\nHi ${customerName},\n\nThanks for your purchase! Your Account Key unlocks your credit wallet — your credits are loaded and ready to use.\n\nACCOUNT KEY: ${licenseKey}\nStarting balance: ${credits} credits\n\nQuick Start:\n1. Open ${productName}\n2. Go to Settings → License\n3. Paste your Account Key: ${licenseKey}\n4. Start using HyperWhisper Cloud\n\nCredits are valid for 12 months from purchase. You can top up anytime from your dashboard at https://hyperwhisper.com/user/dashboard.\n\nNeed help?\nEmail us at ${supportEmail} or visit our customer portal at https://hyperwhisper.com/user.\n\nThis email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because a purchase was made for ${productName}.\n`;\n};\n===== nextjs/lib/templates/credit-topup-email.ts\nimport { escapeHtml } from \"./escape-html\";\n\nexport interface CreditTopUpEmailData {\n  customerName: string;\n  customerEmail: string;\n  licenseKey: string;\n  /** Credits added by this purchase. */\n  creditAmount: number;\n  /** Total spendable balance after this purchase. */\n  newBalance: number;\n  productName: string;\n  supportEmail: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Receipt email for a top-up: credits were added to an existing license key.\n * Confirms the amount added and the new balance.\n */\nexport const creditTopUpEmailHtml = (data: CreditTopUpEmailData) => {\n  const { licenseKey, productName, supportEmail } = data;\n  const customerName = escapeHtml(data.customerName);\n  const added = data.creditAmount.toLocaleString();\n  const balance = data.newBalance.toLocaleString();\n  // Show only a short prefix of the key in the receipt; the full key already\n  // lives on the customer's dashboard and original key email.\n  const keyHint = `${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}…`;\n\n  return `\n<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n    <title>${added} credits added</title>\n</head>\n<body style=\"font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 1.6; color: #333; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 20px; background-color: #ffffff;\">\n    <div style=\"background-color: #f9fafb; border-radius: 8px; padding: 32px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <h1 style=\"color: #2563eb; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 24px;\">Credits added</h1>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 8px; color: #4b5563;\">Hi ${customerName},</p>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 16px; color: #4b5563;\">Thanks for your top-up! We've added credits to your Account Key.</p>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #eff6ff; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; margin: 24px 0; border-left: 4px solid #2563eb;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #1e40af;\">Credits added: <strong>${added}</strong></p>\n            <p style=\"margin: 8px 0 0 0; color: #1e40af;\">New balance: <strong>${balance} credits</strong></p>\n            <p style=\"margin: 8px 0 0 0; color: #1e40af; font-size: 14px;\">Account Key: <span style=\"font-family: 'Courier New', monospace;\">${keyHint}</span></p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #fffbeb; border-radius: 8px; padding: 16px; margin-top: 24px; border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #92400e; font-size: 14px;\">These credits are valid for 12 months from this purchase. See your full balance and history on your <a href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/user/dashboard\" style=\"color: #92400e;\">dashboard</a>.</p>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <p style=\"color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <strong>Need help?</strong> Email us at <a href=\"mailto:${supportEmail}\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">${supportEmail}</a> or visit our <a href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/user\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">customer portal</a>.\n    </p>\n\n    <hr style=\"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e5e7eb; margin: 24px 0;\">\n    <p style=\"color: #9ca3af; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;\">This email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because a purchase was made for ${productName}.</p>\n    <p style=\"font-size: 11px; color: #9ca3af; text-align: center; margin: 24px 0 0 0;\">\n        Ray Amjad LTD<br />\n        Lytchett House, 13 Freeland Park, Wareham Road, Poole, Dorset, BH16 6FA<br />\n        <a href=\"mailto:hello@hyperwhisper.com\" style=\"color: #9ca3af;\">hello@hyperwhisper.com</a>\n    </p>\n</body>\n</html>\n`;\n};\n\nexport const creditTopUpEmailText = (data: CreditTopUpEmailData) => {\n  const { customerName, licenseKey, productName, supportEmail } = data;\n  const added = data.creditAmount.toLocaleString();\n  const balance = data.newBalance.toLocaleString();\n  const keyHint = `${licenseKey.substring(0, 7)}…`;\n\n  return `\nCredits added\n\nHi ${customerName},\n\nThanks for your top-up! We've added credits to your Account Key.\n\nCredits added: ${added}\nNew balance: ${balance} credits\nAccount Key: ${keyHint}\n\nThese credits are valid for 12 months from this purchase. See your full balance and history on your dashboard at https://hyperwhisper.com/user/dashboard.\n\nNeed help?\nEmail us at ${supportEmail} or visit our customer portal at https://hyperwhisper.com/user.\n\nThis email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because a purchase was made for ${productName}.\n`;\n};\n===== nextjs/lib/templates/escape-html.ts\n/**\n * Escape a string for safe interpolation into HTML email markup.\n *\n * Values such as the customer name originate from attacker-controllable\n * sources (e.g. Stripe checkout `customer_details.name`, where a buyer sets\n * their own billing name). Interpolating them raw into the email HTML allows\n * markup/script/style injection into a transactional email sent from a trusted\n * sender. Always escape user-derived values before embedding them in HTML.\n */\nexport const escapeHtml = (value: string): string =>\n    value\n        .replace(/&/g, \"&amp;\")\n        .replace(/</g, \"&lt;\")\n        .replace(/>/g, \"&gt;\")\n        .replace(/\"/g, \"&quot;\")\n        .replace(/'/g, \"&#39;\");\n===== nextjs/lib/templates/license-email.ts\nimport { escapeHtml } from \"./escape-html\";\n\nexport interface LicenseEmailData {\n    customerName: string;\n    customerEmail: string;\n    licenseKey: string;\n    productName: string;\n    downloadUrl?: string;\n    supportEmail: string;\n}\n\nexport const licenseEmailHtml = (data: LicenseEmailData) => {\n    const { licenseKey, productName, downloadUrl, supportEmail } = data;\n    // Escape attacker-controllable values (e.g. Stripe customer_details.name)\n    // before embedding them in the email HTML to prevent markup injection.\n    const customerName = escapeHtml(data.customerName);\n\n    return `\n<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang=\"en\">\n<head>\n    <meta charset=\"utf-8\">\n    <meta name=\"viewport\" content=\"width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0\">\n    <title>Your ${productName} Account Key</title>\n</head>\n<body style=\"font-family: -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, 'Segoe UI', Roboto, 'Helvetica Neue', Arial, sans-serif; line-height: 1.6; color: #333; max-width: 600px; margin: 0 auto; padding: 20px; background-color: #ffffff;\">\n    <div style=\"background-color: #f9fafb; border-radius: 8px; padding: 32px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <h1 style=\"color: #2563eb; margin-top: 0; margin-bottom: 16px; font-size: 24px;\">Welcome to ${productName}!</h1>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 8px; color: #4b5563;\">Hi ${customerName},</p>\n        <p style=\"margin-bottom: 16px; color: #4b5563;\">Thank you for purchasing ${productName}! Your Account Key is ready and waiting for you below.</p>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #eff6ff; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; margin: 24px 0; border-left: 4px solid #2563eb;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #1e40af; font-weight: 600;\">Your Account Key</p>\n            <div style=\"margin-top: 12px; background-color: #dbeafe; padding: 12px; border-radius: 4px;\">\n                <p style=\"margin: 0; font-family: 'Courier New', monospace; font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; color: #1e40af; letter-spacing: 2px; word-break: break-all; text-align: center;\">${licenseKey}</p>\n            </div>\n            <p style=\"margin: 12px 0 0 0; color: #1e40af; font-size: 14px;\">Keep this key safe - you'll need it to activate ${productName}</p>\n        </div>\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #f3f4f6; border-radius: 8px; padding: 20px; margin: 24px 0;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0 0 12px 0; color: #374151; font-weight: 600; font-size: 18px;\">Quick Start Guide</p>\n            <ol style=\"margin: 0; padding-left: 20px; color: #4b5563;\">\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Open ${productName} on your Mac</li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Click on Settings → License</li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Click \"Enter License Key\"</li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Paste your Account Key: <strong>${licenseKey}</strong></li>\n                <li style=\"margin: 8px 0;\">Click \"Activate\" to unlock all Pro features</li>\n            </ol>\n        </div>\n\n        ${downloadUrl\n            ? `<a href=\"${downloadUrl}\" style=\"display: inline-block; background-color: #2563eb; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 600;\">Download ${productName}</a>`\n            : \"\"\n        }\n\n        <div style=\"background-color: #fef3c7; border-radius: 8px; padding: 16px; margin-top: 24px; border-left: 4px solid #f59e0b;\">\n            <p style=\"margin: 0; color: #92400e;\"><strong>Pro Tip:</strong> You can enter your key in the app under <strong>Settings → License</strong> (the HyperWhisper Cloud panel).</p>\n        </div>\n    </div>\n\n    <p style=\"color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">\n        <strong>Need help?</strong> Email us at <a href=\"mailto:${supportEmail}\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">${supportEmail}</a> or visit our <a href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/user\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">customer portal</a> to access your Account Key, receipt, and invoice.\n    </p>\n\n    <hr style=\"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e5e7eb; margin: 24px 0;\">\n    <p style=\"color: #9ca3af; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;\">This email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because a purchase was made for ${productName}.</p>\n    <p style=\"font-size: 11px; color: #9ca3af; text-align: center; margin: 24px 0 0 0;\">\n        Ray Amjad LTD<br />\n        Lytchett House, 13 Freeland Park, Wareham Road, Poole, Dorset, BH16 6FA<br />\n        <a href=\"mailto:hello@hyperwhisper.com\" style=\"color: #9ca3af;\">hello@hyperwhisper.com</a>\n    </p>\n</body>\n</html>\n`;\n};\n\nexport const licenseEmailText = (data: LicenseEmailData) => {\n    const { customerName, licenseKey, productName, downloadUrl, supportEmail } =\n        data;\n\n    return `\nWelcome to ${productName}!\n\nHi ${customerName},\n\nThank you for purchasing ${productName}! Your Account Key is ready:\n\nACCOUNT KEY: ${licenseKey}\n\nQuick Start Guide:\n1. Open ${productName} on your Mac\n2. Click on Settings → License\n3. Click \"Enter License Key\"\n4. Real humans read every message.</li>\n            </ul>\n        </div>\n\n        <a href=\"${downloadUrl}\" style=\"display: inline-block; background-color: #2563eb; color: white; padding: 12px 24px; text-decoration: none; border-radius: 6px; font-weight: 600;\">Download ${productName}</a>\n    </div>\n\n    <a href=\"${loomVideoUrl}\" style=\"display: block; margin-bottom: 24px; border-radius: 8px; overflow: hidden;\" target=\"_blank\" rel=\"noopener noreferrer\">\n        <img src=\"${loomThumbnailUrl}\" alt=\"Watch the HyperWhisper quickstart video\" style=\"display: block; width: 100%; height: auto; border-radius: 8px;\" />\n    </a>\n\n    <p style=\"color: #6b7280; font-size: 14px; margin-bottom: 24px;\">Need a hand? ${supportEmail ? `Email us at <a href=\"mailto:${supportEmail}\" style=\"color: #2563eb;\">${supportEmail}</a>` : \"Just hit reply\"} and we will reply as soon as possible.</p>\n\n    <hr style=\"border: none; border-top: 1px solid #e5e7eb; margin: 24px 0;\">\n    <p style=\"color: #9ca3af; font-size: 12px; margin: 0;\">This email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because you requested a ${productName} download link. There is no need to unsubscribe. If you didn't request this, simply ignore it.</p>\n    <p style=\"font-size: 11px; color: #9ca3af; text-align: center; margin: 24px 0 0 0;\">\n        Ray Amjad LTD<br />\n        Lytchett House, 13 Freeland Park, Wareham Road, Poole, Dorset, BH16 6FA<br />\n        <a href=\"mailto:hello@hyperwhisper.com\" style=\"color: #9ca3af;\">hello@hyperwhisper.com</a>\n    </p>\n</body>\n</html>\n`;\n};\n\n/**\n * Plain-text fallback that keeps the same call-to-action in a transport-safe\n * format for clients that ignore or strip HTML content.\n */\nexport const welcomeEmailText = (data: WelcomeEmailData) => {\n  const { customerName, productName, downloadUrl, loomVideoUrl, supportEmail } =\n    data;\n\n  return `\nWelcome aboard, ${customerName}!\n\nWe just saved your email, so here is everything you need to get started with ${productName}.\n\nGet set up in minutes:\n- Download the application using the bottom below.\n- Watch our quick Loom walkthrough of the transcription workflow.\n- Reply if you have questions and we will help you out.\n\nDownload ${productName}: ${downloadUrl}\n\nWatch the quickstart video: ${loomVideoUrl}\n\nNeed a hand?\n${supportEmail ? `Email us at ${supportEmail}` : \"Reply to this message\"}\n\nThis email was sent to ${data.customerEmail} because you requested a ${productName} download link. from \"@trpc/server\";\n18\t\n19\timport { createTRPCRouter, publicProcedure } from \"../trpc\";\n20\timport { upsertEmail } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n21\timport { disposableDomains } from \"@/lib/disposable_domains\";\n22\timport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\n23\timport { downloadEmailRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\n24\timport { getCountryFromIP } from \"@/lib/services/geolocation\";\n25\timport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"./download-ip\";\n26\t\n27\timport type { WelcomeEmailData } from \"@/lib/templates/welcome-email\";\n28\t\n29\t// ============================================================================\n30\t// HELPER FUNCTIONS\n31\t// ============================================================================\n32\t\n33\tlet cachedDisposableDomains: Set<string> | null = null;\n34\tlet lastLoadMs = 0;\n35\tconst RELOAD_INTERVAL_MS = 1000 * 60 * 60; // 1 hour\n36\t\n37\t/**\n38\t * Get the disposable domains list (cached for 1 hour).\n39\t * Used to block temporary email addresses.\n40\t */\n41\tasync function getDisposableDomains(): Promise<Set<string>> {\n42\t  const now = Date.now();\n43\t\n44\t  if (cachedDisposableDomains && now - lastLoadMs < RELOAD_INTERVAL_MS) {\n45\t    return cachedDisposableDomains;\n46\t  }\n47\t\n48\t  const lines = disposableDomains\n49\t    .map((domain) => domain.trim().toLowerCase())\n50\t    .filter(Boolean);\n51\t\n52\t  cachedDisposableDomains = new Set(lines);\n53\t  lastLoadMs = now;\n54\t\n55\t  return cachedDisposableDomains;\n56\t}\n57\t\n58\t/**\n59\t * Check if a domain is disposable.\n60\t * Walks up the domain tree to catch subdomains.\n61\t *\n62\t * Example: sub.mailinator.com matches mailinator.com in list\n63\t */\n64\tfunction isDisposableDomain(emailDomain: string, list: Set<string>): boolean {\n65\t  if (list.size === 0) return false;\n66\t  let current = emailDomain.toLowerCase();\n67\t\n68\t  // Walk up the domain tree: sub.a.b -> a.b -> b\n69\t  while (true) {\n70\t    if (list.has(current)) return true;\n71\t    const dotIndex = current.indexOf(\".\");\n72\t\n73\t    if (dotIndex === -1) break;\n74\t    const next = current.slice(dotIndex + 1);\n75\t\n76\t    if (!next.includes(\".\")) break; // avoid matching bare TLDs\n77\t    current = next;\n78\t  }\n79\t\n80\t  return false;\n81\t}\n82\t\n83\t/**\n84\t * Fetch and parse appcast.xml to get latest download URL.\n85\t * Swaps hostname to CDN (builds-cdn.hyperwhisper.com).\n86\t */\n87\tasync function getLatestDownloadUrl(origin: string): Promise<string | null> {\n88\t  try {\n89\t    const appcastUrl = `${origin}/appcast.xml`;\n90\t    const res = await fetch(appcastUrl, { cache: \"no-store\" });\n91\t\n92\t    if (!res.ok) return null;\n93\t\n94\t    const xml = await res.text();\n95\t    const match = xml.match(/<item>[\\s\\S]*?<enclosure[^>]*url=\"([^\"]+)\"/i);\n96\t\n97\t    if (!match || !match[1]) return null;\n98\t\n99\t    const latestUrl = new URL(match[1]);\n100\t    latestUrl.hostname = \"builds-cdn.hyperwhisper.com\";\n101\t\n102\t    return latestUrl.toString();\n103\t  } catch (error) {\n104\t    console.error(\"Error parsing appcast:\", error);\n105\t    return null;\n106\t  }\n107\t}\n108\t\n109\t// ============================================================================\n110\t// ROUTER\n111\t// ============================================================================\n112\t\n113\texport const downloadRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n114\t  /**\n115\t   * Get the latest download URL from appcast.xml.\n116\t   */\n117\t  getLatestUrl: publicProcedure.query(async () => {\n118\t    // Use production URL as default since we don't have request context\n119\t    const origin = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n120\t    const downloadUrl = await getLatestDownloadUrl(origin);\n121\t\n122\t    if (!downloadUrl) {\n123\t      throw new TRPCError({\n124\t        code: \"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR\",\n125\t        message: \"Failed to get latest download URL\",\n126\t      });\n127\t    }\n128\t\n129\t    return { downloadUrl };\n130\t  }),\n131\t\n132\t  /**\n133\t   * Record a download with email tracking.\n134\t   */\n135\t  recordDownload: publicProcedure\n136\t    .input(\n137\t      z.object({\n138\t        email: z.string().email(\"Invalid email format\"),\n139\t      })\n140\t    )\n141\t    .mutation(async ({ input, ctx }) => {\n142\t      try {\n143\t        // Rate limiting: 10 requests per IP per hour\n144\t        const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(ctx.headers);\n145\t        const { success } = await downloadEmailRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n146\t\n147\t        if (!success) {\n148\t          throw new TRPCError({\n149\t            code: \"TOO_MANY_REQUESTS\",\n150\t            message: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\",\n151\t          });\n152\t        }\n153\t\n154\t        // Block disposable email domains\n155\t        const domain = String(input.email).split(\"@\")[1]?.toLowerCase();\n156\t        if (!domain) {\n157\t          throw new TRPCError({\n158\t            code: \"BAD_REQUEST\",\n159\t            message: \"Invalid email domain\",\n160\t          });\n161\t        }\n162\t        const domainList = await getDisposableDomains();\n163\t        if (isDisposableDomain(domain, domainList)) {\n164\t          throw new TRPCError({\n165\t            code: \"BAD_REQUEST\",\n166\t            message:\n167\t              \"Disposable email domains are not allowed. Please use a valid email so we can send your download link.\",\n168\t          });\n169\t        }\n170\t\n171\t        // Geolocate the IP for social proof display\n172\t        let country: string | null = null;\n173\t        try {\n174\t          country = await getCountryFromIP(clientIP);\n175\t        } catch {\n176\t          // Geolocation is best-effort; don't block the download\n177\t        }\n178\t\n179\t        // Store email in database (ignore if already exists)\n180\t        try {\n181\t          await upsertEmail({\n182\t            email: input.email,\n183\t            source: \"hyperwhisper-download\",\n184\t            ipAddress: clientIP !== \"unknown\" ? clientIP : null,\n185\t            userAgent: ctx.headers.get(\"user-agent\") || null,\n186\t            country,\n187\t          });\n188\t        } catch (insertError) {\n189\t          console.error(\"Error storing email:\", insertError);\n190\t          // Don't fail the request if email storage fails\n191\t        }\n192\t\n193\t        // Log for monitoring\n194\t        console.log(\"Download requested by:\", input.email);\n195\t\n196\t        const origin = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n197\t        const emailDownloadUrl = `${origin}/api/download`;\n198\t\n199\t        const welcomeEmailPayload: WelcomeEmailData = {\n200\t          customerName: input.email.split(\"@\")[0] || \"HyperWhisper user\",\n201\t          customerEmail: input.email,\n202\t          productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n203\t          downloadUrl: `${origin}/download`,\n204\t          loomVideoUrl:\n205\t            \"https://www.loom.com/share/fd73e59755f9473b8bde341845c402e9?sid=e1892b2e-3ce6-4542-b512-31777a400909\",\n206\t          loomThumbnailUrl:\n207\t            \"https://www.hyperwhisper.com/email-assets/welcome-email-thumbnail.png\",\n208\t          supportEmail: process.env.SUPPORT_EMAIL ?? emailService.sendWelcomeEmail(welcomeEmailPayload);\n214\t\n215\t          if (!welcomeResult.success) {\n216\t            console.error(\n217\t              \"Welcome email failed to send:\",\n218\t              welcomeResult.error ?? \"Unknown error\"\n219\t            );\n220\t          }\n221\t        } catch (err) {\n222\t          console.error(\"Error sending welcome email:\", err);\n223\t        }\n224\t\n225\t        const directDownloadUrl = await getLatestDownloadUrl(origin);\n226\t\n227\t        return {\n228\t          success: true,\n229\t          message: \"Email recorded * Download Router\n *\n * Handles download URL generation and email recording for HyperWhisper downloads.\n * All procedures are public (anyone can download).\n *\n * PROCEDURES:\n * - getLatestUrl: Parses appcast.xml to get latest DMG download URL\n * - recordDownload: Records email, stores in database, sends welcome email\n *\n * INTEGRATIONS:\n * - Database: Email storage (emails table) via db-layer\n * - Resend: Welcome email sending\n * - appcast.xml: Version/download URL source\n */\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\nimport { TRPCError } from \"@trpc/server\";\n\nimport { createTRPCRouter, publicProcedure } from \"../trpc\";\nimport { upsertEmail } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport { disposableDomains } from \"@/lib/disposable_domains\";\nimport { emailService } from \"@/lib/services/email\";\nimport { downloadEmailRateLimiter } from \"@/lib/rate-limit\";\nimport { getCountryFromIP } from \"@/lib/services/geolocation\";\nimport { getClientIPFromHeaders } from \"./download-ip\";\n\nimport type { WelcomeEmailData } from \"@/lib/templates/welcome-email\";\n\n// ============================================================================\n// HELPER FUNCTIONS\n// ============================================================================\n\nlet cachedDisposableDomains: Set<string> | null = null;\nlet lastLoadMs = 0;\nconst RELOAD_INTERVAL_MS = 1000 * 60 * 60; // 1 hour\n\n/**\n * Get the disposable domains list (cached for 1 hour).\n * Used to block temporary email addresses.\n */\nasync function getDisposableDomains(): Promise<Set<string>> {\n  const now = Date.now();\n\n  if (cachedDisposableDomains && now - lastLoadMs < RELOAD_INTERVAL_MS) {\n    return cachedDisposableDomains;\n  }\n\n  const lines = disposableDomains\n    .map((domain) => domain.trim().toLowerCase())\n    .filter(Boolean);\n\n  cachedDisposableDomains = new Set(lines);\n  lastLoadMs = now;\n\n  return cachedDisposableDomains;\n}\n\n/**\n * Check if a domain is disposable.\n * Walks up the domain tree to catch subdomains.\n *\n * Example: sub.mailinator.com matches mailinator.com in list\n */\nfunction isDisposableDomain(emailDomain: string, list: Set<string>): boolean {\n  if (list.size === 0) return false;\n  let current = emailDomain.toLowerCase();\n\n  // Walk up the domain tree: sub.a.b -> a.b -> b\n  while (true) {\n    if (list.has(current)) return true;\n    const dotIndex = current.indexOf(\".\");\n\n    if (dotIndex === -1) break;\n    const next = current.slice(dotIndex + 1);\n\n    if (!next.includes(\".\")) break; // avoid matching bare TLDs\n    current = next;\n  }\n\n  return false;\n}\n\n/**\n * Fetch and parse appcast.xml to get latest download URL.\n * Swaps hostname to CDN (builds-cdn.hyperwhisper.com).\n */\nasync function getLatestDownloadUrl(origin: string): Promise<string | null> {\n  try {\n    const appcastUrl = `${origin}/appcast.xml`;\n    const res = await fetch(appcastUrl, { cache: \"no-store\" });\n\n    if (!res.ok) return null;\n\n    const xml = await res.text();\n    const match = xml.match(/<item>[\\s\\S]*?<enclosure[^>]*url=\"([^\"]+)\"/i);\n\n    if (!match || !match[1]) return null;\n\n    const latestUrl = new URL(match[1]);\n    latestUrl.hostname = \"builds-cdn.hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    return latestUrl.toString();\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Error parsing appcast:\", error);\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n// ============================================================================\n// ROUTER\n// ============================================================================\n\nexport const downloadRouter = createTRPCRouter({\n  /**\n   * Get the latest download URL from appcast.xml.\n   */\n  getLatestUrl: publicProcedure.query(async () => {\n    // Use production URL as default since we don't have request context\n    const origin = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n    const downloadUrl = await getLatestDownloadUrl(origin);\n\n    if (!downloadUrl) {\n      throw new TRPCError({\n        code: \"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR\",\n        message: \"Failed to get latest download URL\",\n      });\n    }\n\n    return { downloadUrl };\n  }),\n\n  /**\n   * Record a download with email tracking.\n   */\n  recordDownload: publicProcedure\n    .input(\n      z.object({\n        email: z.string().email(\"Invalid email format\"),\n      })\n    )\n    .mutation(async ({ input, ctx }) => {\n      try {\n        // Rate limiting: 10 requests per IP per hour\n        const clientIP = getClientIPFromHeaders(ctx.headers);\n        const { success } = await downloadEmailRateLimiter.limit(clientIP);\n\n        if (!success) {\n          throw new TRPCError({\n            code: \"TOO_MANY_REQUESTS\",\n            message: \"Too many requests. Please try again later.\",\n          });\n        }\n\n        // Block disposable email domains\n        const domain = String(input.email).split(\"@\")[1]?.toLowerCase();\n        if (!domain) {\n          throw new TRPCError({\n            code: \"BAD_REQUEST\",\n            message: \"Invalid email domain\",\n          });\n        }\n        const domainList = await getDisposableDomains();\n        if (isDisposableDomain(domain, domainList)) {\n          throw new TRPCError({\n            code: \"BAD_REQUEST\",\n            message:\n              \"Disposable email domains are not allowed. Please use a valid email so we can send your download link.\",\n          });\n        }\n\n        // Geolocate the IP for social proof display\n        let country: string | null = null;\n        try {\n          country = await getCountryFromIP(clientIP);\n        } catch {\n          // Geolocation is best-effort; don't block the download\n        }\n\n        // Store email in database (ignore if already exists)\n        try {\n          await upsertEmail({\n            email: input.email,\n            source: \"hyperwhisper-download\",\n            ipAddress: clientIP !== \"unknown\" ? clientIP : null,\n            userAgent: ctx.headers.get(\"user-agent\") || null,\n            country,\n          });\n        } catch (insertError) {\n          console.error(\"Error storing email:\", insertError);\n          // Don't fail the request if email storage fails\n        }\n\n        // Log for monitoring\n        console.log(\"Download requested by:\", input.email);\n\n        const origin = process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n        const emailDownloadUrl = `${origin}/api/download`;\n\n        const welcomeEmailPayload: WelcomeEmailData = {\n          customerName: input.email.split(\"@\")[0] || \"HyperWhisper user\",\n          customerEmail: input.email,\n          productName: \"HyperWhisper\",\n          downloadUrl: `${origin}/download`,\n          loomVideoUrl:\n            \"https://www.loom.com/share/fd73e59755f9473b8bde341845c402e9?sid=e1892b2e-3ce6-4542-b512-31777a400909\",\n          loomThumbnailUrl:\n            \"https://www.hyperwhisper.com/email-assets/welcome-email-thumbnail.png\",\n          supportEmail: process.env.SUPPORT_EMAIL ?? \"support@hyperwhisper.com\",\n        };\n\n        try {\n          const welcomeResult =\n            await emailService.sendWelcomeEmail(welcomeEmailPayload);\n\n          if (!welcomeResult.success) {\n            console.error(\n              \"Welcome email failed to send:\",\n              welcomeResult.error ?? \"Unknown error\"\n            );\n          }\n        } catch (err) {\n          console.error(\"Error sending welcome email:\", err);\n        }\n\n        const directDownloadUrl = await getLatestDownloadUrl(origin);\n\n        return {\n          success: true,\n          message: \"Email recorded successfully\",\n          downloadUrl: directDownloadUrl || emailDownloadUrl,\n        };\n      } catch (error) {\n        console.error(\"Error processing download request:\", error);\n\n        if (error instanceof TRPCError) {\n          throw error;\n        }\n\n        throw new TRPCError({\n          code: \"INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR\",\n          message: \"Internal server error\",\n        });\n      }\n    }),\n});\n","numLines":246,"startLine":1,"totalLines":246}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"18d0e95b-8e62-44af-bb62-6f3e800909ab","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Carries the Resend error name/statusCode so we can decide whether\n33\t * the failure is worth retrying.\n34\t */\n35\tclass ResendSendError extends Error {\n36\t  readonly retryable: boolean;\n37\t\n38\t  constructor(message: string, name: string, statusCode: number | null) {\n39\t    super(message);\n40\t    this.name = \"ResendSendError\";\n41\t    this.retryable = isRetryableResendError(name, statusCode);\n42\t  }\n43\t}\n44\t\n45\t/**\n46\t * Decide whether a Resend failure is transient (worth retrying) or permanent.\n47\t *\n48\t * Permanent failures (invalid recipient/domain, validation errors, auth errors,\n49\t * suppressed recipients) will never succeed on retry, so we fail fast instead of\n50\t * burning the webhook request thread on backoff sleeps.\n51\t */\n52\tfunction isRetryableResendError(\n53\t  name: string | undefined,\n54\t  statusCode: number | null,\n55\t): boolean {\n56\t  // Rate limits and server-side errors are transient.\n57\t  if (\n58\t    name === \"rate_limit_exceeded\" ||\n59\t    name === \"internal_server_error\" ||\n60\t    name === \"application_error\"\n61\t  ) {\n62\t    return true;\n63\t  }\n64\t\n65\t  // Any 5xx (or unknown/missing status) is treated as transient.\n66\t  if (statusCode == null) return true;\n67\t  return statusCode >= 500;\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\texport class EmailService {\n71\t  private static instance: EmailService;\n72\t  private readonly maxRetries = 3;\n73\t  // Base backoff. Kept small because retries run inline inside the Stripe\n74\t  // webhook request; worst-case total sleep across attempts is bounded\n75\t  // (250ms + 500ms = 750ms) to stay well under Stripe's webhook timeout.\n76\t  private readonly retryDelay = 250;\n77\t\n78\t  private constructor() {}\n79\t\n80\t  public static getInstance(): EmailService {\n81\t    if (!EmailService.instance) {\n82\t { resend, DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL } from \"@/lib/clients/resend\";\nimport {\n  licenseEmailHtml,\n  licenseEmailText,\n  type LicenseEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/license-email\";\nimport {\n from \"@/lib/templates/credit-mint-email\";\nimport {\n  creditTopUpEmailHtml,\n  creditTopUpEmailText,\n  type CreditTopUpEmailData,\n} from \"@/lib/templates/credit-topup-email\";\nimport { logSentEmail } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\nexport interface EmailResult {\n  success: boolean;\n  data?: any;\n  error?: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Error thrown when the Resend API resolves with an error object instead of\n * throwing. Carries the Resend error name/statusCode so we can decide whether\n * the failure is worth retrying.\n */\nclass ResendSendError extends Error {\n  readonly retryable: boolean;\n\n  constructor(message: string, name: string, statusCode: number | null) {\n    super(message);\n    this.name = \"ResendSendError\";\n    this.retryable = isRetryableResendError(name, statusCode);\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Decide whether a Resend failure is transient (worth retrying) or permanent.\n *\n * Permanent failures (invalid recipient/domain, validation errors, auth errors,\n * suppressed recipients) will never succeed on retry, so we fail fast instead of\n * burning the webhook request thread on backoff sleeps.\n */\nfunction isRetryableResendError(\n  name: string | undefined,\n  statusCode: number | null,\n): boolean {\n  // Rate limits and server-side errors are transient.\n  if (\n    name === \"rate_limit_exceeded\" ||\n    name === \"internal_server_error\" ||\n    name === \"application_error\"\n  ) {\n    return true;\n  }\n\n  // Any 5xx (or unknown/missing status) is treated as transient.\n  if (statusCode == null) return true;\n  return statusCode >= 500;\n}\n\nexport class EmailService {\n  private static instance: EmailService;\n  private readonly maxRetries = 3;\n  // Base backoff. Kept small because retries run inline inside the Stripe\n  // webhook request; worst-case total sleep across attempts is bounded\n  // (250ms + 500ms = 750ms) to stay well under Stripe's webhook timeout.\n  private readonly retryDelay = 250;\n\n  private constructor() {}\n\n  public static getInstance(): EmailService {\n    if (!EmailService.instance) {\n      EmailService.instance = new EmailService();\n    }\n\n    return EmailService.instance;\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send license key email with retry logic\n   */\n  async sendLicenseKey(data: LicenseEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Your ${data.productName} License Key`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"license\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: licenseEmailHtml(data),\n        text: licenseEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the onboarding welcome email used after someone submits the download form.\n   */\n  async sendWelcomeEmail(data: WelcomeEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Welcome to ${data.productName}`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"welcome\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: welcomeEmailHtml(data),\n        text: welcomeEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the mint email: a guest bought credits with no key, so we created one.\n   * Delivers the new key and its starting balance.\n   */\n  async sendCreditMint(data: CreditMintEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `Your ${data.productName} key and credits`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"credit-mint\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: creditMintEmailHtml(data),\n        text: creditMintEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Send the top-up receipt: credits were added to an existing license key.\n   */\n  async sendCreditTopUp(data: CreditTopUpEmailData): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    const subject = `${data.creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits added`;\n    return this.sendWithRetry(\"credit-topup\", data.customerEmail, subject, () =>\n      resend.emails.send({\n        from: DEFAULT_FROM_EMAIL,\n        to: data.customerEmail,\n        subject,\n        html: creditTopUpEmailHtml(data),\n        text: creditTopUpEmailText(data),\n      }),\n    );\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Shared send-with-retry loop.\n   *\n   * Treats a resolved `{ error }` object from the Resend SDK as a failure (the\n   * SDK does NOT throw on API-level errors), so transient failures actually\n   * retry and permanent failures surface as `{ success: false }` instead of a\n   * false positive. Permanent (non-retryable) errors abort immediately so the\n   * caller's request thread is not blocked on backoff sleeps.\n   */\n  private async sendWithRetry(\n    kind: \"license\" | \"welcome\" | \"credit-mint\" | \"credit-topup\",\n    customerEmail: string,\n    subject: string,\n    send: () => Promise<{ data: unknown; error: unknown }>,\n  ): Promise<EmailResult> {\n    let lastError: Error | undefined;\n\n    for (let attempt = 1; attempt <= this.maxRetries; attempt++) {\n      try {\n        console.log(\n          `Sending ${kind} email to ${customerEmail} (attempt ${attempt}/${this.maxRetries})`,\n        );\n\n        const result = await send();\n\n        // The Resend SDK resolves to { data, error } and does NOT throw on\n        // API-level failures, so an error object must be inspected explicitly.\n        if (result.error) {\n          const err = result.error as {\n            message?: string;\n            name?: string;\n            statusCode?: number | null;\n          };\n          throw new ResendSendError(\n            err.message || `Resend returned an error sending ${kind} email`,\n            err.name ?? \"\",\n            err.statusCode ?? null,\n          );\n        }\n\n        console.log(`${capitalize(kind)} email sent successfully to ${customerEmail}`);\n\n        await this.safeLogSentEmail({\n          recipient: customerEmail,\n          emailType: kind,\n          subject,\n          providerMessageId: (result.data as { id?: string })?.id ?? null,\n          status: \"sent\",\n        });\n\n        return { success: true, data: result.data };\n      } catch (error) {\n        lastError = error as Error;\n        console.error(\n          `Failed to send ${kind} email (attempt ${attempt}):`,\n          error,\n        );\n\n        // Don't retry permanent failures (bad recipient/domain, validation,\n        // auth) — they will never succeed and would only block on backoff.\n        if (error instanceof ResendSendError && !error.retryable) {\n          break;\n        }\n\n        // If not the last attempt, wait before retrying (exponential backoff).\n        if (attempt < this.maxRetries) {\n          const delay = this.retryDelay * Math.pow(2, attempt - 1);\n\n          console.log(`Retrying in ${delay}ms...`);\n          await this.sleep(delay);\n        }\n      }\n    }\n\n    await this.safeLogSentEmail({\n      recipient: customerEmail,\n      emailType: kind,\n      subject,\n      status: \"failed\",\n      errorMessage: lastError?.message ?? null,\n    });\n\n    return {\n      success: false,\n      error:\n        lastError?.message || \"Failed to send email after multiple attempts\",\n    };\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Best-effort audit log of one logical email. A logging failure must never\n   * affect email delivery or its result, so this swallows and console.errors —\n   * both the success and failure paths route through here so the behavior can't\n   * drift between them.\n   */\n  private async safeLogSentEmail(\n    data: Parameters<typeof logSentEmail>[0],\n  ): Promise<void> {\n    try {\n      await logSentEmail(data);\n    } catch (logError) {\n      console.error(\n        `Failed to log ${data.status} ${data.emailType} email:`,\n        logError,\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  /**\n   * Sleep for a specified duration\n   */\n  private sleep(ms: number): Promise<void> {\n    return new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, ms));\n  }\n}\n\nfunction capitalize(value: string): string {\n  return value.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + value.slice(1);\n}\n\n// Export singleton instance\nexport const emailService = EmailService.getInstance();\n","numLines":275,"startLine":1,"totalLines":275}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c6f321dd-004e-4551-aefa-756b1bb3e414","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Rejects protocol-relative (\"//evil.com\") and\n * backslash (\"/\\\\evil.com\") prefixes that satisfy a naive `startsWith(\"/\")`\n * check but resolve to an attacker-controlled origin, preventing open redirects.\n */\nexport function sanitizeReturnTo(\n  returnTo: string | null | undefined,\n  fallback: string,\n) {\n  if (\n    returnTo &&\n    returnTo.startsWith(\"/\") &&\n    !returnTo.startsWith(\"//\") &&\n    !returnTo.startsWith(\"/\\\\\")\n  ) {\n    return returnTo;\n  }\n\n  return fallback;\n}\n\nexport function sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL: string | undefined) {\n  return sanitizeReturnTo(callbackURL, DEFAULT_LICENSE_KEY_REDIRECT);\n}\n","numLines":28,"startLine":1,"totalLines":28}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"a63c545e-81da-4139-be89-8c15c00b225b","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

createMiddleware from \"next-intl/middleware\";\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { routing } from \"./src/i18n/routing\";\nimport { defaultLocale, locales } from \"./src/i18n/locales\";\nimport { sanitizeReturnTo } from \"./src/lib/license-key-redirect\";\n\nconst intlMiddleware = createMiddleware(routing);\nconst localePattern = locales\n  .map((locale) => locale.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\\]\\\\]/g, \"\\\\$&\"))\n  .join(\"|\");\nconst LOCALE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})(\\\\/|$)`);\nconst USER_ROUTE_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user`);\nconst USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/sign-in`);\nconst USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX = new RegExp(\n  `^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/auth\\\\/sign-out`,\n);\nconst USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX = new RegExp(`^\\\\/(${localePattern})\\\\/user\\\\/customers`);\n\nconst getPathLocale = (pathname: string) => {\n  const match = pathname.match(LOCALE_REGEX);\n  return match?.[1] ?? defaultLocale;\n};\n\n/**\n * Check if Better Auth session cookie is present.\n * For middleware, we only check cookie presence (no HTTP round-trip).\n * Actual session validation happens in the API layer.\n *\n * Better Auth prefixes cookies with \"__Secure-\" in production when\n * baseURL starts with \"https://\", so we check both names.\n */\nfunction hasSessionCookie(request: NextRequest): boolean {\n  return !!(\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\")?.value ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\")?.value\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Get full Better Auth session via API call.\n * Only used for admin routes that need the user's email.\n */\nasync function getBetterAuthSession(request: NextRequest) {\n  const sessionCookie =\n    request.cookies.get(\"__Secure-better-auth.session_token\") ||\n    request.cookies.get(\"better-auth.session_token\");\n  if (!sessionCookie?.value) return null;\n\n  try {\n    const baseUrl = request.nextUrl.origin;\n    const response = await fetch(`${baseUrl}/api/auth/get-session`, {\n      headers: {\n        cookie: request.headers.get(\"cookie\") || \"\",\n      },\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) return null;\n\n    const data = await response.json();\n    return data?.user ?? null;\n  } catch {\n    return null;\n  }\n}\n\n/**\n * Middleware that handles:\n * 1. next-intl locale routing (adds locale prefix)\n * 2. Better Auth session checking\n * 3. User route protection (unified portal for customers and admins)\n */\nexport default async function middleware(request: NextRequest) {\n  const { pathname } = request.nextUrl;\n\n  // Check if this is a user route (matches /<locale>/user/*)\n  const isUserRoute = USER_ROUTE_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserSignIn = USER_SIGN_IN_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserAuthSignOut = USER_AUTH_SIGN_OUT_REGEX.test(pathname);\n  const isUserCustomers = USER_CUSTOMERS_REGEX.test(pathname);\n\n  // =============================================================\n  // USER ROUTES - Unified portal for customers and admins\n  // =============================================================\n\n  // For /user/customers, require admin access\n  if (isUserCustomers) {\n    const user = await getBetterAuthSession(request);\n\n    // If not authenticated, redirect to user sign-in\n    if (!user) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // Must be admin to access customers page\n    if (user.role !== \"admin\") {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(\n        new URL(`/${locale}/user/dashboard`, request.url)\n      );\n    }\n\n    // User is authenticated and is admin - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For other user routes (except sign-in, sign-out), check authentication via cookie presence\n  if (\n    isUserRoute &&\n    !isUserSignIn &&\n    !isUserAuthSignOut\n  ) {\n    if (!hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const signInUrl = new URL(`/${locale}/user/sign-in`, request.url);\n      signInUrl.searchParams.set(\"returnTo\", pathname);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(signInUrl);\n    }\n\n    // User has session cookie - run intl middleware\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For user sign-in page, check if already authenticated\n  if (isUserSignIn) {\n    if (hasSessionCookie(request)) {\n      const locale = getPathLocale(pathname);\n      const returnTo = request.nextUrl.searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n      const redirectUrl = sanitizeReturnTo(returnTo, `/${locale}/user/dashboard`);\n      return NextResponse.redirect(new URL(redirectUrl, request.url));\n    }\n\n    const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n    response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n    return response;\n  }\n\n  // For all other routes, just run intl middleware\n  const response = intlMiddleware(request) as NextResponse;\n  response.headers.set(\"x-pathname\", pathname);\n\n  return response;\n}\n\nexport const config = {\n  // Match all pathnames except for:\n  // - API routes (/api/*)\n  // - Raw model inventory endpoint (/models)\n  // - Next.js internal files (/_next/*)\n  // - Static files with extensions (*.*)\n  // - Documentation (/docs/*)\n  matcher: [\"/((?!api|models(?:/|$)|_next|_vercel|docs|.*\\\\..*).*)\"],\n};\n","numLines":161,"startLine":1,"totalLines":161}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"ea9d1ecd-8fa9-47c9-81a0-e6a998a8c50a","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

License Key — enter HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX directly\n * 2. Email — magic link authentication via Better Auth\n *\n * Note: Any email can sign in. Admin features are shown based on\n * email being in the admin allowlist (checked in layout/middleware).\n */\nexport default function UserSignInPage() {\n  const router = useRouter();\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const params = useParams();\n  const locale = (params.locale as string) || \"en\";\n\n  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<Tab>(\"license-key\");\n  const [autoSigningIn, setAutoSigningIn] = useState(false);\n\n  // License key tab state\n  const [licenseKey, setLicenseKey] = useState(\"\");\n  const [licenseSubmitting, setLicenseSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [licenseError, setLicenseError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  // Email tab state\n  const [email, setEmail] = useState(\"\");\n  const [emailSubmitting, setEmailSubmitting] = useState(false);\n  const [magicLinkSent, setMagicLinkSent] = useState(false);\n  const [emailError, setEmailError] = useState<string | null>(null);\n\n  function getCallbackURL() {\n    const returnTo = searchParams.get(\"returnTo\");\n    return sanitizeReturnTo(returnTo, `/${locale}/user/dashboard`);\n  }\n\n  const licenseKeyParam = searchParams.get(\"licenseKey\");\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (!licenseKeyParam) return;\n    setLicenseKey(licenseKeyParam);\n    setAutoSigningIn(true);\n    handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(licenseKeyParam);\n  }, []); // eslint-disable-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps\n\n  async function handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(key: string) {\n    setLicenseError(null);\n    setLicenseSubmitting(true);\n    try {\n      const callbackURL = getCallbackURL();\n      const res = await fetch(\"/api/auth/sign-in/license-key\", {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        headers: { \"Content-Type\": \"application/json\" },\n        body: JSON.stringify({ licenseKey: key.trim(), callbackURL }),\n      });\n      const data = await res.json();\n      if (!res.ok) {\n        setLicenseError(data.error ?? \"Sign-in failed. Please try again.\");\n        setAutoSigningIn(false);\n      } else {\n        router.push(data.redirect);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      setLicenseError(\"An unexpected error occurred. Please try again.\");\n      setAutoSigningIn(false);\n    } finally {\n      setLicenseSubmitting(false);\n    }\n  }\n\n  async function handleLicenseKeySignIn(e?: React.FormEvent) {\n    e?.preventDefault();\n    await handleLicenseKeySignInWithKey(licenseKey);\n  }\n\n  async function handleSendMagicLink(e?: React.FormEvent) {\n    e?.preventDefault();\n    setEmailError(null);\n    setEmailSubmitting(true);\n\n    try {\n      const callbackURL = getCallbackURL();\n      const { error: authError } = await authClient.signIn.magicLink({\n        email,\n        callbackURL,\n      });\n\n      if (authError) {\n        setEmailError(authError.message ?? \"Failed to send magic link. Please try again.\");\n      } else {\n        setMagicLinkSent(true);\n      }\n    } catch {\n      setEmailError(\"An unexpected error occurred. {"parentUuid":"13cbfda4-331f-4108-97d5-94275e81e53d","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_016RGK3WA7QKNyP4gaC1qhsQ","type":"tool_result","content":"// WEBSOCKET STREAMING ROUTE\n// GET /ws/streaming-deepgram - Deepgram Live proxy\n\nimport type { Context, Next } from 'hono';\nimport { upgradeWebSocket } from 'hono/bun';\nimport { generateRequestId, getClientIP } from '../lib/request-id';\nimport { computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost, creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { validateAuth, type AuthContext } from '../middleware/auth';\nimport { deductCredits, validateCredits } from '../middleware/credits';\nimport { isIPBlocked } from '../lib/redis';\n\ninterface DeepgramLiveResponse {\n  type: string;\n  duration?: number;\n  is_final?: boolean;\n  speech_final?: boolean;\n  channel?: {\n    alternatives?: Array<{ transcript?: string }>;\n  };\n}\n\ninterface ReadyMessage {\n  type: 'ready';\n  sessionId: string;\n}\n\ninterface TranscriptMessage {\n  type: 'transcript';\n  text: string;\n  is_final: boolean;\n  speech_final: boolean;\n}\n\ninterface SessionCompleteMessage {\n  type: 'session_complete';\n  duration_seconds: number;\n  credits_used: number;\n}\n\ninterface ErrorMessage {\n  type: 'error';\n  message: string;\n}\n\ntype ServerMessage = ReadyMessage | TranscriptMessage | SessionCompleteMessage | ErrorMessage;\n\nconst STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE = 16000;\nconst STREAMING_CHANNELS = 1;\nconst LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE = 2;\n\n// Minimum balance required to open a streaming session (~30s of Deepgram Nova-3 audio).\nconst STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS = 30;\n\nexport function minimumStreamingCredits(): number {\n  return creditsForCost(computeDeepgramTranscriptionCost(STREAMING_MIN_BALANCE_SECONDS));\n}\n\n// Inbound audio limits — guard the Deepgram proxy against a misbehaving or\n// malicious client that pushes binary far faster (or larger) than the natural\n// 32 KB/s rate of 16 kHz mono linear16. Without these caps the client can grow\n// the outbound socket's buffer unbounded until the Fly machine OOMs.\n//\n// A single audio frame above 1 MB (~32 s of audio) is abnormal for streaming.\nconst MAX_AUDIO_MESSAGE_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n// Cumulative per-session cap (~52 min of 16 kHz mono linear16), well above any\n// real dictation session. Exceeding it closes the socket with 1009.\nconst MAX_SESSION_AUDIO_BYTES = 100 * 1024 * 1024;\n// If the upstream socket has more than this still buffered, the client is\n// outrunning Deepgram — drop the chunk instead of queueing more memory.\nconst MAX_DEEPGRAM_BUFFERED_BYTES = 1 * 1024 * 1024;\n\ninterface WSContext {\n  readyState: number;\n  send(data: string | ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): void;\n  close(code?: number, reason?: string): void;\n}\n\ndeclare module 'hono' {\n  interface ContextVariableMap {\n    wsAuth: AuthContext;\n    wsClientIP: string;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction sendToClient(socket: WSContext, message: ServerMessage): void {\n  if (socket.readyState === 1) {\n    socket.send(JSON.stringify(message));\n  }\n}\n\nexport function durationSecondsForLinear16AudioBytes(byteLength: number): number {\n  return byteLength / (STREAMING_SAMPLE_RATE * STREAMING_CHANNELS * LINEAR16_BYTES_PER_SAMPLE);\n}\n\nfunction buildDeepgramUrl(language?: string, vocabulary?: string): string {\n  const params = new URLSearchParams({\n    model: 'nova-3',\n    smart_format: 'true',\n    interim_results: 'true',\n    punctuate: 'true',\n    endpointing: '300',\n    encoding: 'linear16',\n    sample_rate: '16000',\n    channels: '1',\n    mip_opt_out: 'true',\n  });\n\n  const normalizedLanguage = language?.toLowerCase();\n  if (normalizedLanguage && normalizedLanguage !== 'auto') {\n    params.set('language', normalizedLanguage);\n    if (vocabulary) {\n      // Nova-3 `keyterm` takes ONE repeated query value per term — `keyterm=a&\n      // keyterm=b` — and does NOT support a `:boost` suffix (that's the legacy\n      // nova-2 `keywords` syntax). A single comma-joined `a:1.5,b:1.5` value\n      // boosts one literal phrase and does nothing. Split/clean to match the\n      // REST adapter's convertToKeyterms, then append each term individually.\n      const terms = vocabulary\n        .split(/[,\\n;]+/)\n        .map(t => t.trim().replace(/^[-*]\\s*/, ''))\n        .filter(t => t.length > 0 && t.length <= 50)\n        .slice(0, 100);\n      for (const term of terms) {\n        params.append('keyterm', term);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;\n}\n\nexport async function wsStreamingPreflight(c: Context, next: Next) {\n  const upgradeHeader = c.req.header('Upgrade');\n  if (!upgradeHeader || upgradeHeader.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') {\n    return c.text('Expected WebSocket upgrade', 426);\n  }\n\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return c.text('Access denied', 403);\n  }","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"0b750a20-9059-4771-90df-c51b047e7280","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:35.986Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"// WEBSOCKET STREAMING ROUTE\n// GET /ws/streaming-deepgram - Split/clean to match the\n      // REST adapter's convertToKeyterms, then append each term individually.\n      const terms = vocabulary\n        .split(/[,\\n;]+/)\n        .map(t => t.trim().replace(/^[-*]\\s*/, ''))\n        .filter(t => t.length > 0 && t.length <= 50)\n        .slice(0, 100);\n      for (const term of terms) {\n        params.append('keyterm', term);\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n  return `wss://api.deepgram.com/v1/listen?${params.toString()}`;\n}\n\nexport async function wsStreamingPreflight(c: Context, next: Next) {\n  const upgradeHeader = c.req.header('Upgrade');\n  if (!upgradeHeader || upgradeHeader.toLowerCase() !== 'websocket') {\n    return c.text('Expected WebSocket upgrade', 426);\n  }\n\n  const clientIP = getClientIP(c);\n  if (await isIPBlocked(clientIP)) {\n    return c.text('Access denied', 403);\n  }","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"13cbfda4-331f-4108-97d5-94275e81e53d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
