{"parentUuid":"01f79c59-2384-4aba-bc1e-3051d41fb56e","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01AHBp8HyJuZEKrLydWx6PBS","type":"tool_result","content":"1\timport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n2\t\n3\timport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\n4\timport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n5\timport {\n6\t  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n7\t  computeCreditPurchase,\n8\t} from \"./validation\";\n9\t\n10\t/**\n11\t * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n12\t *\n13\t * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n14\t * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n15\t * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n16\t * existing key is the other path.\n17\t *\n18\t * REQUEST BODY:\n19\t * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n20\t * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n21\t * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n22\t *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n23\t *\n24\t * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n25\t * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n26\t * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n27\t *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n28\t *\n29\t * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n30\t * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n31\t * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n32\t * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n33\t * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n34\t */\n35\t\n36\texport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n37\t  try {\n38\t    const body = await req.json();\n39\t    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n40\t      licenseKey?: unknown;\n41\t      amount?: unknown;\n42\t      email?: unknown;\n43\t    };\n44\t\n45\t    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n46\t    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n47\t    if (amountError !== null) {\n48\t      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n49\t    }\n50\t\n51\t    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n52\t      amount as number\n53\t    );\n54\t\n55\t    const siteUrl =\n56\t      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n57\t\n58\t    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n59\t    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n60\t    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n61\t    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n62\t    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n63\t    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n64\t      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n65\t        ? licenseKey.trim()\n66\t        : undefined;\n67\t    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n68\t\n69\t    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n70\t    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n71\t    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n72\t    const guestEmail =\n73\t      !hasLicenseKey &&\n74\t      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n75\t      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n76\t        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n77\t        : undefined;\n78\t\n79\t    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n80\t      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n81\t      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n82\t      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n83\t    };\n84\t\n85\t    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n86\t    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n87\t    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n88\t\n89\t    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n90\t      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n91\t\n92\t      if (!license) {\n93\t        console.error(\n94\t          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n95\t          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n96\t        );\n97\t        return NextResponse.json(\n98\t          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n99\t          { status: 400 }\n100\t        );\n101\t      }\n102\t\n103\t      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n104\t      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n105\t      // them.\n106\t      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n107\t        return NextResponse.json(\n108\t          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n109\t          { status: 400 }\n110\t        );\n111\t      }\n112\t\n113\t      const email = license.email;\n114\t      if (!email) {\n115\t        return NextResponse.json(\n116\t          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n117\t          { status: 400 }\n118\t        );\n119\t      }\n120\t\n121\t      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n122\t\n123\t      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n124\t      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n125\t      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n126\t        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n127\t          email,\n128\t          limit: 1,\n129\t        });\n130\t\n131\t        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n132\t          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n133\t        } else {\n134\t          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n135\t            email,\n136\t            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n137\t          });\n138\t          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n139\t        }\n140\t\n141\t        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n142\t      }\n143\t    }\n144\t\n145\t    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n146\t    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n147\t    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n148\t      mode: \"payment\",\n149\t      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n150\t      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n151\t      ...(stripeCustomerId\n152\t        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n153\t        : {\n154\t            customer_creation: \"always\",\n155\t            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n156\t          }),\n157\t      line_items: [\n158\t        {\n159\t          price_data: {\n160\t            currency: \"usd\",\n161\t            product_data: {\n162\t              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n163\t              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n164\t              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n165\t              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n166\t              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n167\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n168\t            },\n169\t            unit_amount: creditCents,\n170\t          },\n171\t          quantity: 1,\n172\t        },\n173\t        {\n174\t          price_data: {\n175\t            currency: \"usd\",\n176\t            product_data: {\n177\t              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n178\t              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n179\t              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n180\t              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n181\t            },\n182\t            unit_amount: feeCents,\n183\t          },\n184\t          quantity: 1,\n185\t        },\n186\t      ],\n187\t\n188\t      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n189\t      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n190\t\n191\t      // Metadata for webhook processing\n192\t      metadata,\n193\t\n194\t      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n195\t      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n196\t\n197\t      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n198\t      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n199\t      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n200\t      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n201\t      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n202\t    });\n203\t\n204\t    // Return checkout URL to client\n205\t    if (session.url) {\n206\t      return NextResponse.json({\n207\t        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n208\t      });\n209\t    } else {\n210\t      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n211\t    }\n212\t  } catch (error) {\n213\t    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n214\t\n215\t    return NextResponse.json(\n216\t      {\n217\t        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n218\t        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n219\t      },\n220\t      { status: 500 }\n221\t    );\n222\t  }\n223\t}\n224\t"}]},"uuid":"1c54cdc4-4aca-455d-b745-1c39dfe275f9","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:42.889Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/nextjs/app/api/checkout/credits/route.ts","content":"import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { stripe } from \"@/lib/clients/stripe\";\nimport { findAccountByKey, updateAccountKey } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\nimport {\n  validateCreditPurchaseAmount,\n  computeCreditPurchase,\n} from \"./validation\";\n\n/**\n * Stripe Checkout API Route for Credit Purchases\n *\n * Creates a Stripe Checkout Session for HyperWhisper credit purchases. The\n * license key IS the wallet, so this is the only way to obtain a key: a guest\n * can buy credits with no key and the webhook mints + emails one. Topping up an\n * existing key is the other path.\n *\n * REQUEST BODY:\n * - amount: whole dollars, 5..500 (1000 credits per $1)\n * - licenseKey (optional): when present, top up that key instead of minting\n * - email (optional): mint path only. Prefills the Stripe checkout email so the\n *   minted key is emailed to that address; ignored when topping up a key.\n *\n * PRICING (two non-bundled line items):\n * - Credits: amount * 100 cents -> amount * 1000 credits\n * - Processing fee (6%): round(amount * 100 * 0.06) cents — revenue, never\n *   granted as credits, and non-refundable.\n *\n * STRIPE METADATA (read by the webhook):\n * - purchase_type: \"credits\"\n * - credit_amount: credits to grant\n * - fee_cents: the processing fee charged\n * - license_key: present ONLY when topping up an existing key (absent => mint)\n */\n\nexport async function POST(req: NextRequest) {\n  try {\n    const body = await req.json();\n    const { licenseKey, amount, email } = body as {\n      licenseKey?: unknown;\n      amount?: unknown;\n      email?: unknown;\n    };\n\n    // Validate amount: whole dollars within [MIN, MAX].\n    const amountError = validateCreditPurchaseAmount(amount);\n    if (amountError !== null) {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: amountError }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n\n    const { creditAmount, creditCents, feeCents } = computeCreditPurchase(\n      amount as number\n    );\n\n    const siteUrl =\n      process.env.NEXT_PUBLIC_SITE_URL || \"https://hyperwhisper.com\";\n\n    // Optional license key: when supplied, this is a top-up of an existing\n    // wallet; when absent, the webhook mints a brand-new key on payment.\n    // Trim first: a whitespace-only key must NOT count as \"present\" (which would\n    // skip the mint path and then fail the key lookup). Normalize once and use the\n    // trimmed value everywhere below.\n    const normalizedLicenseKey =\n      typeof licenseKey === \"string\" && licenseKey.trim().length > 0\n        ? licenseKey.trim()\n        : undefined;\n    const hasLicenseKey = normalizedLicenseKey !== undefined;\n\n    // Mint path only: a guest-supplied email to prefill at checkout. Loose\n    // format check — Stripe re-validates and the buyer can still edit it. The\n    // webhook mints/emails the key off the email Stripe ultimately collects.\n    const guestEmail =\n      !hasLicenseKey &&\n      typeof email === \"string\" &&\n      /^[^\\s@]+@[^\\s@]+\\.[^\\s@]+$/.test(email.trim())\n        ? email.trim().toLowerCase()\n        : undefined;\n\n    const metadata: Record<string, string> = {\n      purchase_type: \"credits\",\n      credit_amount: creditAmount.toString(),\n      fee_cents: feeCents.toString(),\n    };\n\n    // Resolve the Stripe customer for the top-up path so credits attach to the\n    // same customer/email. The mint path lets Stripe collect the email itself.\n    let stripeCustomerId: string | undefined;\n\n    if (normalizedLicenseKey) {\n      const license = await findAccountByKey(normalizedLicenseKey);\n\n      if (!license) {\n        console.error(\n          \"License lookup failed for key:\",\n          normalizedLicenseKey.substring(0, 7)\n        );\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"Invalid license key\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      // Reject non-granted (e.g. revoked) licenses: credits added to them would\n      // be unusable because /api/license/credits refuses to validate or deduct\n      // them.\n      if (license.status !== \"granted\") {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: `License is ${license.status}` },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      const email = license.email;\n      if (!email) {\n        return NextResponse.json(\n          { error: \"License has no email associated\" },\n          { status: 400 }\n        );\n      }\n\n      metadata.license_key = normalizedLicenseKey;\n\n      // Find or create the Stripe customer by email and cache it on the license.\n      stripeCustomerId = license.stripeCustomerId ?? undefined;\n      if (!stripeCustomerId) {\n        const existingCustomers = await stripe.customers.list({\n          email,\n          limit: 1,\n        });\n\n        if (existingCustomers.data.length > 0) {\n          stripeCustomerId = existingCustomers.data[0].id;\n        } else {\n          const newCustomer = await stripe.customers.create({\n            email,\n            metadata: { license_key: normalizedLicenseKey },\n          });\n          stripeCustomerId = newCustomer.id;\n        }\n\n        await updateAccountKey(license.id, { stripeCustomerId });\n      }\n    }\n\n    // Create Stripe Checkout Session\n    // @ts-expect-error - managed_payments is in private preview\n    const session = await stripe.checkout.sessions.create({\n      mode: \"payment\",\n      // Top-up: attach to the resolved customer. Mint: always create a customer\n      // during checkout, prefilling the guest email when one was supplied.\n      ...(stripeCustomerId\n        ? { customer: stripeCustomerId }\n        : {\n            customer_creation: \"always\",\n            ...(guestEmail ? { customer_email: guestEmail } : {}),\n          }),\n      line_items: [\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"HyperWhisper Cloud credits\",\n              description: `${creditAmount.toLocaleString()} credits`,\n              // Managed Payments requires every line item's product to carry a\n              // tax_code. txcd_10000000 = \"General - Electronically Supplied\n              // Services\", which fits prepaid credits for a cloud STT service.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: creditCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n        {\n          price_data: {\n            currency: \"usd\",\n            product_data: {\n              name: \"Cloud processing fee (6%)\",\n              description: \"Non-refundable HyperWhisper Cloud processing fees\",\n              // Same Managed Payments requirement: the fee line needs a tax_code.\n              tax_code: \"txcd_10000000\",\n            },\n            unit_amount: feeCents,\n          },\n          quantity: 1,\n        },\n      ],\n\n      // Allow coupon/promotion codes\n      allow_promotion_codes: true,\n\n      // Metadata for webhook processing\n      metadata,\n\n      // Managed Payments: Stripe handles tax, invoicing, and compliance\n      managed_payments: { enabled: true },\n\n      // Land on the public confirmation page (works for guests, who have no\n      // account yet). It only personalizes copy from `credits` — entitlement is\n      // always enforced server-side via the webhook-minted key.\n      success_url: `${siteUrl}/purchase-success?session_id={CHECKOUT_SESSION_ID}&credits=${creditAmount}`,\n      cancel_url: `${siteUrl}/credits`,\n    });\n\n    // Return checkout URL to client\n    if (session.url) {\n      return NextResponse.json({\n        checkoutUrl: session.url,\n      });\n    } else {\n      throw new Error(\"No checkout URL returned from Stripe\");\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Credit checkout error:\", error);\n\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      {\n        error: \"Failed to create checkout session\",\n        details: error instanceof Error ? error.message : \"Unknown error\",\n      },\n      { status: 500 }\n    );\n  }\n}\n","numLines":224,"startLine":1,"totalLines":224}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"01f79c59-2384-4aba-bc1e-3051d41fb56e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}
{"parentUuid":"3d5ee154-f35f-45e7-ab22-cddbd2e845a1","isSidechain":true,"promptId":"872714b7-1488-4cc4-8bad-5341f8c86fd3","agentId":"a007d4af5c3401e3e","type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"tool_use_id":"toolu_01Pshy28Somi9dhpitdwLTdM","type":"tool_result","content":"// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n// yet. This is the single source of that rule for the raw-SQL credit queries\n// below (balance read, spend, clawback) — keep it here so expiry semantics live\n// in one place. The Drizzle-builder variant (using creditGrants.expiresAt) is\n// expressed inline where the query is built with the query builder.\nconst ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY = sql`(expires_at IS NULL OR expires_at > now())`;\n\n/**\n * Authoritative spendable balance: the SUM of remaining_amount across a\n * license's still-active grants. The credit_grants rows are the source of\n * truth; credit_balances is only a denormalized cache of this value. Reading\n * via the passed executor lets callers inside a FOR UPDATE transaction get a\n * consistent, locked view rather than a stale snapshot from the outer pool.\n */\nasync function getActiveGrantsTotal(\n  executor: DbExecutor,\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const result = await executor.execute<{ total: string | null }>(sql`\n    SELECT COALESCE(SUM(remaining_amount), 0) AS total\n    FROM credit_grants\n    WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n      AND status = 'active'\n      AND remaining_amount > 0\n      AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n  `);\n  return Number(result.rows[0]?.total ?? 0);\n}\n\n/**\n * Force the credit_balances cache to equal the authoritative grant total,\n * reconciling any drift between the two. Returns the reconciled balance. This\n * is the single place that heals divergence (e.g. a legacy balance row with no\n * matching grants, or a partially-applied transaction) so the cache can never\n * permanently show credits the grants can't back, or vice versa.\n */\nasync function reconcileCreditBalance(\n  tx: Parameters<Parameters<typeof db.transaction>[0]>[0],\n  userId: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const total = await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, userId);\n  await tx\n    .insert(creditBalances)\n    .values({ userId, balance: total.toString() })\n    .onConflictDoUpdate({\n      target: creditBalances.userId,\n    // on remaining_amount > 0: a fully-consumed pack still has to be clawed back,\n    // otherwise spending the pack first (so a refund can't hand back unused paid\n    // credits — see spendCreditGrantsByProvenance / #872) would let a refund of a\n    // fully-spent pack reclaim nothing, leaving the user with the paid usage for\n    // free PLUS the cash refund (the inverse money-loss bug).\n    const target = await tx.execute<{\n      id: string;\n      user_id: string;\n      original_amount: string;\n      refunded_amount: string;\n    }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, user_id, original_amount, refunded_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE source_type = ${data.sourceType}\n        AND source_id = ${data.sourceId}\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    const grant = target.rows[0];\n    if (!grant) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // The amount this refund reclaims is the grant's full purchased value that\n    // has not already been refunded. A refunded grant (refunded_amount already\n    // covers original_amount) is a no-op / duplicate webhook delivery.\n    const originalAmount = Number(grant.original_amount);\n    const alreadyRefunded = Number(grant.refunded_amount);\n    const clawback = originalAmount - alreadyRefunded;\n    if (clawback <= 0) {\n      return { status: \"duplicate\", refundedAmount: 0 };\n    }\n\n    // A full refund must remove the FULL purchased value from the license's\n    // spendable balance, regardless of which grant happened to be spent for the\n    // matching usage. Clamped at the license's current active total so a user who\n    // already burned the credits doesn't go negative, but no value escapes:\n    //   spend order + refund clawback are reconciled against the SAME running\n    //   total, so neither bundle-first nor pack-first ordering can leak money.\n    let toClawback = Math.min(\n      clawback,\n      await getActiveGrantsTotal(tx, grant.user_id)\n    );\n\n    // Draw the clawback down from the license's active, unexpired grants,\n    // starting with the refunded grant itself, then the same oldest-first order\n    // used when spending (soonest-to-expire first). The clawback total is\n    // clamped at the license's current active balance, and both spend and\n    // clawback reconcile against that same running total, so no ordering can\n    // leak money. Expired grants are skipped: they no longer back any spendable\n    // balance, so clawing them back would remove value that was never available.\n    const drawdown = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${grant.user_id}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      ORDER BY\n        CASE WHEN id = ${grant.id} THEN 0 ELSE 1 END,\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE\n    `);\n\n    for (const row of drawdown.rows) {\n      if (toClawback <= 0) break;\n      const rowRemaining = Number(row.remaining_amount);\n      const deduction = Math.min(rowRemaining, toClawback);\n      if (deduction <= 0) continue;\n      refundedAmount: clawback,\n    };\n  });\n}\n\nexport async function spendCreditGrantsByProvenance(\n  userId: string,\n  amount: number\n): Promise<{ balance: number; deductedAmount: number }> {\n  return db.transaction(async (tx) => {\n    const result = await tx.execute<{ id: string; remaining_amount: string }>(sql`\n      SELECT id, remaining_amount\n      FROM credit_grants\n      WHERE user_id = ${userId}\n        AND remaining_amount > 0\n        AND status = 'active'\n        AND ${ACTIVE_GRANT_EXPIRY}\n      -- Spend OLDEST-FIRST: soonest-to-expire grants are consumed before grants\n      -- that still have time on them (and never-expiring grants last), so a user\n      -- naturally burns down credits before they lapse. This replaces the older\n      -- provenance (paid-pack-first) order; refund safety is now preserved a\n      -- different way — refundCreditGrant clamps every clawback at the license's\n      -- current active balance and reconciles against the same running total\n      -- (#872 / spec §6), so neither spend nor clawback ordering can leak money\n      -- regardless of which grant a given unit of usage drew from. Expired\n      -- grants are excluded above and never spent.\n      ORDER BY\n        expires_at ASC,\n        created_at ASC,\n        id\n      FOR UPDATE","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"4030b015-8e3f-4d9c-bdc3-5f8c6ba88a07","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:46:44.479Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"// A grant still backs spendable balance when it has no expiry or hasn't expired\n// yet. nextjs/app/api/internal/grant-license/route.ts\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\r\nimport { timingSafeEqualSecret } from \"@/lib/security/timing-safe-secret\";\r\nimport {\r\n  getAccountKeysByEmail,\r\n  provisionAccountKeyForEmail,\r\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\r\n\r\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\r\n  // Validate internal secret\r\n  const secret = request.headers.get(\"x-internal-secret\");\r\n  if (!timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)) {\r\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, { status: 401 });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  let email: string;\r\n  try {\r\n    const body = await request.json();\r\n    email = body?.email;\r\n    if (!email || typeof email !== \"string\") {\r\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: \"email is required\" }, { status: 400 });\r\n    }\r\n    email = email.toLowerCase().trim();\r\n  } catch {\r\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid JSON body\" }, { status: 400 });\r\n  }\r\n\r\n  try {\r\n    // Check for an existing *granted* license by email (most recent first).\r\n    // A revoked/refunded license must not be re-handed-out: it is dead at\r\n    // /api/license/validate and returning it would skip the credit grant below.\r\n    const existing = await getAccountKeysByEmail(email);\r\n    const granted = existing.find((l) => l.status === \"granted\");\r\n    if (granted) {\r\n      return NextResponse.json({ licenseKey: granted.key });\r\n    }\r\n\r\n    // No granted license yet — mint one via the shared internal mint flow.\r\n    const license = await provisionAccountKeyForEmail(email);\r\n    return NextResponse.json({ licenseKey: license.key });\r\n  } catch (error) {\r\n    console.error(\"Error in grant-license:\", error);\r\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }, { status: 500 });\r\n  }\r\n}\r\n===== nextjs/app/api/internal/granted-emails/route.ts\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\nimport { timingSafeEqualSecret } from \"@/lib/security/timing-safe-secret\";\nimport { getGrantedEmails } from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Internal bulk read: returns every distinct normalized email that holds at\n * least one granted Account Key. Powers the ACS admin backfill, which marks\n * those members as having already claimed their credit perk. Read-only; no\n * mutation. Same x-internal-secret gate (timing-safe, 401 on bad secret) as the\n * other internal routes.\n */\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n  const secret = request.headers.get(\"x-internal-secret\");\n  if (!timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    const emails = await getGrantedEmails();\n    return NextResponse.json({ emails });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Error in granted-emails:\", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n===== nextjs/app/api/internal/licenses-for-email/route.ts\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\nimport { timingSafeEqualSecret } from \"@/lib/security/timing-safe-secret\";\nimport {\n  getAccountKeysByEmail,\n  getCreditBalancesForUsers,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\nexport async function POST(request: NextRequest) {\n  // Validate internal secret\n  const secret = request.headers.get(\"x-internal-secret\");\n  if (!timingSafeEqualSecret(secret, process.env.HYPERWHISPER_INTERNAL_SECRET)) {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Unauthorized\" }, { status: 401 });\n  }\n\n  let email: string;\n  try {\n    const body = await request.json();\n    email = body?.email;\n    if (!email || typeof email !== \"string\") {\n      return NextResponse.json({ error: \"email is required\" }, { status: 400 });\n    }\n    email = email.toLowerCase().trim();\n  } catch {\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Invalid JSON body\" }, { status: 400 });\n  }\n\n  try {\n    // Read-only: this endpoint never mints. An email with no granted key simply\n    // returns an empty list. (Minting happens only via grant-license, driven by\n    // the ACS claim flow — so nothing here can accidentally create a key.)\n    const all = await getAccountKeysByEmail(email);\n\n    // Display only active keys; revoked keys are hidden. Credits are pooled per\n    // account, so resolve balances by the keys' distinct owning users; each key\n    // then reports its account balance.\n    const granted = all.filter((l) => l.status === \"granted\");\n    const balances = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(\n      Array.from(new Set(granted.map((l) => l.userId)))\n    );\n\n    const licenses = granted.map((license) => ({\n      key: license.key,\n      status: license.status,\n      createdAt: license.createdAt.toISOString(),\n      credits: balances.get(license.userId) ?? 0,\n    }));\n\n    return NextResponse.json({ licenses });\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.error(\"Error in licenses-for-email:\", error);\n    return NextResponse.json({ error: \"Internal server error\" }, { status: 500 });\n  }\n}\n===== nextjs/app/api/internal/models/route.ts\nimport { NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { fetchAvailableModels } from \"@/lib/services/model-list\";\n\nexport const dynamic = \"force-dynamic\";\nexport const runtime = \"nodejs\";\n\nexport async function GET() {\n  return NextResponse.json(await fetchAvailableModels(), {\n    headers: {\n      // Let Vercel's edge cache absorb anonymous traffic without invoking the\n      // function (which would fan out paid provider calls). Pairs with the\n      // ~1h in-memory cache in fetchAvailableModels() for cache-busted requests.\n      \"Cache-Control\": \"public, s-maxage=3600, stale-while-revalidate=86400\",\n    },\n Used only to\n  // personalize the confirmation copy — never trusted for entitlement.\n  const creditsParam = searchParams?.get(\"credits\");\n  const credits = creditsParam ? Number.parseInt(creditsParam, 10) : NaN;\n  const hasCredits = Number.isFinite(credits) && credits > 0;\n\n  useEffect(() => {\n    // Support both Stripe (session_id) and legacy Polar (checkout_id) params\n    const id =\n      searchParams?.get(\"session_id\") || searchParams?.get(\"checkout_id\");\n\n    if (id) {\n      setCheckoutId(id);\n    }\n  }, [searchParams]);\n\n  // Separate effect to capture purchase event only when PostHog is ready\n  useEffect(() => {\n    if (checkoutId && posthog && !eventCaptured) {\n      posthog.capture(\"purchase_completed\", { session_id: checkoutId });\n      setEventCaptured(true);\n    }\n  }, [checkoutId, posthog, eventCaptured]);\n\n  const steps = [\n    { title: t(\"step1Title\"), description: t(\"step1Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step2Title\"), description: t(\"step2Desc\") },\n    { title: t(\"step3Title\"), description: t(\"step3Desc\") },\n  ];\n\n  return (\n    <div className=\"min-h-screen bg-gradient-to-b from-gray-900 via-purple-900/10 to-gray-900 flex items-center justify-center px-7 py-20\">\n      <m.div\n        animate={{ opacity: 1, scale: 1 }}\n        className=\"max-w-xl w-full text-center\"\n        initial={{ opacity: 0, scale: 0.95 }}\n        transition={{ duration: 0.5 }}\n      >\n        <m.div\n          animate={{ scale: 1 }}\n          className=\"inline-flex items-center justify-center w-[82px] h-[82px] bg-gradient-to-r from-green-500 to-emerald-500 rounded-full mb-9 shadow-[0_12px_40px_-8px_rgba(16,185,129,0.5)]\"\n=== credits page ===\nimport { redirect } from \"next/navigation\";\n\nimport CreditsPurchase from \"@/components/credits/CreditsPurchase\";\n\n/**\n * Credits Page\n *\n * Default (no params): the guest buy-credits flow — enter an email, pick an\n * amount, and check out. The webhook mints a license key (the wallet) and emails\n * it. See components/credits/CreditsPurchase.tsx.\n *\n * Native-app deep links still carry a key and route through sign-in so the user\n * lands on their dashboard to top up an existing wallet:\n *   - ?license_key=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX (macOS, Windows)\n *   - ?id=HW-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX-XXXX          (legacy macOS — kept for older app versions)\n */\nexport default async function CreditsPage({\n  params,\n  searchParams,\n}: {\n  params: Promise<{ locale: string }>;\n  searchParams: Promise<{ id?: string; license_key?: string }>;\n}) {\n  const { locale } = await params;\n  const { id, license_key } = await searchParams;\n  const raw = id ?? license_key ?? \"\";\n\n  if (raw.startsWith(\"HW-\")) {\n    redirect(\n      `/${locale}/user/sign-in?licenseKey=${encodeURIComponent(raw)}&returnTo=/${locale}/user/dashboard`\n    );\n  }\n\n  return (\n    <main className=\"min-h-screen bg-black px-6 py-24 md:py-28\">\n      <CreditsPurchase locale={locale} />\n    </main>\n  );\n}","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"c6a507b9-c62d-45fe-bec5-c590c8b6b110","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:14.477Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== purchase-success ===\nnextjs/app/[locale]/purchase-success/layout.tsx\nnextjs/app/[locale]/purchase-success/page.tsx\n\"use client\";\n\nimport { useEffect, useState, Suspense } from \"react\";\nimport { m } from \"framer-motion\";\nimport { Button } from \"@heroui/button\";\nimport { CheckCircle, ArrowRight } from \"lucide-react\";\nimport { useSearchParams } from \"next/navigation\";\nimport { usePostHog } from \"posthog-js/react\";\nimport { useTranslations } from \"next-intl\";\n\nimport { Link } from \"@/src/i18n/navigation\";\n\nfunction PurchaseSuccessContent() {\n  const searchParams = useSearchParams();\n  const posthog = usePostHog();\n  const t = useTranslations(\"purchaseSuccess\");\n  const [checkoutId, setCheckoutId] = useState<string | null>(null);\n  const [eventCaptured, setEventCaptured] = useState(false);\n\n  // Credits purchased, passed through the Stripe success_url.                        href={dl.downloadUrl}\n=== config route ===\nimport { NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\n// DEPRECATED: local trial limits were removed (HyperWhisper is open source —\n// local transcription and model downloads are unconditionally free and\n// unlimited). New desktop builds no longer fetch or enforce these values, but\n// legacy / un-upgraded clients still in the field DO fetch this endpoint and\n// apply the returned numbers as live trial caps. So instead of the old\n// 300s/day + 3-model gate, we return effectively-unlimited values to keep\n// those legacy clients from gating local use.\n//\n// Why 2_000_000_000 specifically (not i64::MAX or 0):\n//   - Legacy Windows ConfigService parses these as 32-bit `int`, so i64::MAX\n//     overflows and breaks JSON deserialization. 2_000_000_000 fits in int32\n//     (max 2,147,483,647) with headroom; macOS parsed as 64-bit and is fine.\n//   - The Rust core enforces via `used >= limit` / `remaining = (limit-used)`,\n//     so 0 or a negative value would block everything. A large positive value\n//     is effectively unlimited (~63 years of seconds / 2B models) with no\n//     underflow.\n// Safe to delete once those legacy versions are no longer in the field.\nexport async function GET() {\n  return NextResponse.json(\n    {\n      trial_daily_limit_seconds: 2_000_000_000,\n      trial_model_download_limit: 2_000_000_000,\n    },\n    {\n      headers: {\n        \"Cache-Control\": \"public, max-age=21600\", // 6 hours\n      },\n    }\n  );\n}\n=== customer profile ===\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { auth } from \"@/src/lib/auth\";\nimport {\n  getAccountKeysByEmail,\n  getCreditBalancesForUsers,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Customer Profile API\n *\n * Returns the authenticated customer's profile and license information.\n *\n * GET /api/customer/profile\n * Returns:\n * - user: { id, email }\n * - licenses: Array of license objects with credits\n * - totalCredits: Sum of all credits across licenses\n *\n * Requires authentication via Better Auth session cookie.\n */\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: req.headers });\n  if (!session?.user) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Unauthorized\" },\n      { status: 401 }\n    );\n  }\n  const user = session.user;\n\n  try {\n    const userEmail = user.email?.toLowerCase() ?? \"\";\n\n    // Fetch user's license keys from database\n    const licenses = await getAccountKeysByEmail(userEmail);\n\n    // Credits are pooled per account. Resolve balances by the licenses' distinct\n    // owning users; each license then reports its account balance.\n    const userIds = Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)));\n    const creditMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds);\n\n    // Add credits to each license object\n    const licensesWithCredits = licenses.map((license) => ({\n      id: license.id,\n      key: license.key,\n      status: license.status,\n      created_at: license.createdAt.toISOString(),\n      stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId,\n      polar_customer_id: license.polarCustomerId,","is_error":false}]},"uuid":"58570051-f80c-4504-a3d3-956621707bb3","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:47:57.102Z","toolUseResult":{"stdout":"=== href={ / src={ dynamic A large positive value\n//     is effectively unlimited (~63 years of seconds / 2B models) with no\n//     underflow.\n// Safe to delete once those legacy versions are no longer in the field.\nexport async function GET() {\n  return NextResponse.json(\n    {\n      trial_daily_limit_seconds: 2_000_000_000,\n      trial_model_download_limit: 2_000_000_000,\n    },\n    {\n      headers: {\n        \"Cache-Control\": \"public, max-age=21600\", // 6 hours\n      },\n    }\n  );\n}\n=== customer profile ===\nimport { NextRequest, NextResponse } from \"next/server\";\n\nimport { auth } from \"@/src/lib/auth\";\nimport {\n  getAccountKeysByEmail,\n  getCreditBalancesForUsers,\n} from \"@/src/lib/db-layer\";\n\n/**\n * Customer Profile API\n *\n * Returns the authenticated customer's profile and license information.\n *\n * GET /api/customer/profile\n * Returns:\n * - user: { id, email }\n * - licenses: Array of license objects with credits\n * - totalCredits: Sum of all credits across licenses\n *\n * Requires authentication via Better Auth session cookie.\n */\nexport async function GET(req: NextRequest) {\n  const session = await auth.api.getSession({ headers: req.headers });\n  if (!session?.user) {\n    return NextResponse.json(\n      { error: \"Unauthorized\" },\n      { status: 401 }\n    );\n  }\n  const user = session.user;\n\n  try {\n    const userEmail = user.email?.toLowerCase() ?? \"\";\n\n    // Fetch user's license keys from database\n    const licenses = await getAccountKeysByEmail(userEmail);\n\n    // Credits are pooled per account. Resolve balances by the licenses' distinct\n    // owning users; each license then reports its account balance.\n    const userIds = Array.from(new Set(licenses.map((l) => l.userId)));\n    const creditMap = await getCreditBalancesForUsers(userIds);\n\n    // Add credits to each license object\n    const licensesWithCredits = licenses.map((license) => ({\n      id: license.id,\n      key: license.key,\n      status: license.status,\n      created_at: license.createdAt.toISOString(),\n      stripe_customer_id: license.stripeCustomerId,\n      polar_customer_id: license.polarCustomerId,","stderr":"","interrupted":false,"isImage":false,"noOutputExpected":false},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"b672b6e7-9650-4c72-b372-7816efd0e20d","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

{ createAuthEndpoint } from \"better-auth/api\";\nimport { setSessionCookie } from \"better-auth/cookies\";\nimport { eq } from \"drizzle-orm\";\nimport { z } from \"zod\";\n\nimport { findAccountByKey } from \"./db-layer\";\nimport { sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect } from \"./license-key-redirect\";\n\nimport { db } from \"@/src/db\";\nimport { user } from \"@/src/db/schema/auth\";\n\nexport const licenseKeyPlugin = () => ({\n  id: \"license-key\",\n  endpoints: {\n    signInLicenseKey: createAuthEndpoint(\n      \"/sign-in/license-key\",\n      {\n        method: \"POST\",\n        requireHeaders: true,\n        body: z.object({\n          licenseKey: z.string(),\n          callbackURL: z.string().optional(),\n        }),\n      },\n      async (ctx) => {\n        const { licenseKey, callbackURL } = ctx.body;\n\n        const license = await findAccountByKey(licenseKey);\n\n        if (!license || license.status !== \"granted\") {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Invalid or inactive license key.\" },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        if (!license.userId) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const [foundUser] = await db\n          .select()\n          .from(user)\n          .where(eq(user.id, license.userId))\n          .limit(1);\n\n        if (!foundUser) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            {\n              error:\n                \"No account found for this license key. Please contact support.\",\n            },\n            { status: 400 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        const session = await ctx.context.internalAdapter.createSession(\n          foundUser.id,\n        );\n\n        if (!session) {\n          return ctx.json(\n            { error: \"Failed to create session.\" },\n            { status: 500 },\n          );\n        }\n\n        await setSessionCookie(ctx, { session, user: foundUser });\n\n        const ipAddress =\n          ctx.headers?.get(\"x-forwarded-for\")?.split(\",\")[0]?.trim() ??\n          \"unknown\";\n        const userAgent = ctx.headers?.get(\"user-agent\") ?? \"unknown\";\n        console.log(\n          `[license-key-sign-in] user=${foundUser.id} license=${license.id} ip=${ipAddress} ua=\"${userAgent}\"`,\n        );\n\n        return ctx.json({ redirect: sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect(callbackURL) });\n      },\n    ),\n  },\n  rateLimit: [\n    {\n      pathMatcher(path: string) {\n        return path === \"/sign-in/license-key\";\n      },\n      window: 60,\n      max: 5,\n    },\n  ],\n});\n","numLines":98,"startLine":1,"totalLines":98}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"4fb89949-99f7-4731-b1b4-32e89c1fe7ee","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n50\t\n51\t    try {\n52\t      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n53\t    } catch {\n54\t      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n55\t    }\n56\t\n57\t    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n58\t    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n59\t\n60\t    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n61\t\n62\t    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n63\t    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n64\t    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n65\t    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n66\t    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n67\t    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n68\t    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n69\t    // the licensing API on every call.\n70\t    if (response.status >= 500) {\n71\t      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n72\t      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n73\t    }\n74\t\n75\t    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n76\t      isValid,\n77\t      credits,\n78\t      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n79\t    });\n80\t\n81\t    return { isValid, credits };\n82\t  } catch (error) {\n83\t    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n84\t    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. };\n145\t}\n146\t"}]},"uuid":"87a2a654-a5ae-4094-a79c-39012d6a6b26","timestamp":"2026-07-29T11:48:19.354Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"/Users/ray/Desktop/hyperwhisper-public/hyperwhisper-cloud/src/middleware/auth.ts","content":"// AUTHENTICATION HELPERS\n// Validates license keys and device trial identifiers\n\nimport { DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { cacheLicense, getCachedLicense } from '../lib/redis';\nimport { invalidLicenseResponse, licenseRequiredResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n\nexport interface AuthContext {\n  identifier: string; // license key\n  credits: number;\n  licenseKey: string;\n}\n\nexport interface AuthInput {\n  licenseKey?: string;\n}\n\nexport type AuthResult =\n  | { ok: true; value: AuthContext }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\n// Mask license key for logging (show first 4 and last 4 chars)\nfunction maskLicenseKey(key: string): string {\n  if (key.length <= 8) return '****';\n  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}...${key.slice(-4)}`;\n}\n\nasync function validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey: string): Promise<{ isValid: boolean; credits: number }> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n  const validateUrl = `${apiBase}/api/license/validate`;\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  console.log(`[License] Validating ${maskedKey} via ${validateUrl}`);\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(validateUrl, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        include_credits: true,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    const responseText = await response.text();\n    let data: { valid?: boolean; credits?: number; error?: string } = {};\n\n    try {\n      data = JSON.parse(responseText);\n    } catch {\n      console.error(`[License] Invalid JSON response for ${maskedKey}: ${responseText.slice(0, 200)}`);\n    }\n\n    const isValid = data.valid === true;\n    const credits = typeof data.credits === 'number' ? data.credits : 0;\n\n    console.log(`[License] ${maskedKey}: status=${response.status}, valid=${isValid}, credits=${credits}${data.error ? `, error=${data.error}` : ''}`);\n\n    // A 5xx (cold start, upstream timeout, internal error) is a transient\n    // failure, not proof the license is invalid — caching it would lock a\n    // paying user out for the full LICENSE_CACHE_TTL_SECONDS. Fail this\n    // request closed but leave the cache untouched so the next request\n    // retries the API. A 4xx, by contrast, is a definitive verdict from the\n    // licensing API (revoked/not-found/malformed key → valid:false); those\n    // MUST be cached so repeated requests with the same bad key don't hammer\n    // the licensing API on every call.\n    if (response.status >= 500) {\n      console.warn(`[License] Transient ${response.status} response for ${maskedKey}; not caching`);\n      return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n    }\n\n    await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n      isValid,\n      credits,\n      cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n    });\n\n    return { isValid, credits };\n  } catch (error) {\n    // Network/DNS/timeout failure — we could not reach the license API, so we\n    // cannot conclude the license is invalid. Do NOT cache: caching invalid\n    // here would lock a paying user out for the full TTL. Fail this request\n    // closed; the next request retries against the API.\n    if (error instanceof DOMException && (error.name === 'TimeoutError' || error.name === 'AbortError')) {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation timed out for ${maskedKey} after ${LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS}ms (transient, not cached)`);\n    } else {\n      console.error(`[License] Validation failed for ${maskedKey} (transient, not cached):`, error);\n    }\n    return { isValid: false, credits: 0 };\n  }\n}\n\nexport async function validateAuth(input: AuthInput, forceRefresh = false): Promise<AuthResult> {\n  const { licenseKey } = input;\n\n  // HyperWhisper Cloud is licensed-only: a valid license key (which carries the\n  // credit balance) is required for every request. There is no anonymous/trial\n  // path — without a key the request is rejected before any provider work.\n  if (!licenseKey) {\n    console.log('[Auth] No license_key provided');\n    return { ok: false, response: licenseRequiredResponse() };\n  }\n\n  const maskedKey = maskLicenseKey(licenseKey);\n\n  if (!forceRefresh) {\n    const cached = await getCachedLicense(licenseKey);\n    if (cached) {\n      console.log(`[Auth] Cache HIT for ${maskedKey}: valid=${cached.isValid}, credits=${cached.credits}, cachedAt=${cached.cachedAt}`);\n      if (!cached.isValid) {\n        return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n      }\n      return {\n        ok: true,\n        value: {\n          identifier: licenseKey,\n          credits: cached.credits,\n          licenseKey,\n        },\n      };\n    }\n    console.log(`[Auth] Cache MISS for ${maskedKey}, calling API...`);\n  } else {\n    console.log(`[Auth] Force refresh for ${maskedKey}, bypassing cache...`);\n  }\n\n  const validation = await validateLicenseViaApi(licenseKey);\n  if (!validation.isValid) {\n    console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is INVALID`);\n    return { ok: false, response: invalidLicenseResponse() };\n  }\n\n  console.log(`[Auth] License ${maskedKey} is VALID with ${validation.credits} credits`);\n  return {\n    ok: true,\n    value: {\n      identifier: licenseKey,\n      credits: validation.credits,\n      licenseKey,\n    },\n  };\n}\n","numLines":146,"startLine":1,"totalLines":146}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"f9979b0c-906d-40e6-86a5-2c304fb07ad7","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n2\t// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n3\t\n4\timport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\n5\timport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\n6\timport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\n7\timport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\n8\timport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\n9\timport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n10\t\n11\t// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\n12\tconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n13\t\n14\texport type CreditsResult =\n15\t  | { ok: true }\n16\t  | { ok: false; response: Response CREDIT VALIDATION + DEDUCTION\n// Handles preflight credit checks and post-usage deduction\n\nimport type { AuthContext } from './auth';\nimport { BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE, CREDITS_PER_MINUTE, DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL, LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS } from '../lib/constants';\nimport { roundToTenth, roundUpToTenth } from '../lib/utils';\nimport { creditsForCost } from '../lib/cost-calculator';\nimport { insufficientCreditsResponse } from '../lib/responses';\nimport { cacheLicense } from '../lib/redis';\n\n// Minimum encoded bitrate expected from clients for conservative credit estimation.\nconst MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS = 10;\n\nexport type CreditsResult =\n  | { ok: true }\n  | { ok: false; response: Response };\n\nexport interface CreditEstimateOptions {\n  costEstimators?: Array<(durationSeconds: number) => number>;\n}\n\nexport function estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes: number): number {\n  const estimatedMinutes = sizeBytes / BYTES_PER_MINUTE_ESTIMATE;\n  return Math.max(MIN_ESTIMATED_SECONDS, estimatedMinutes * 60);\n}\n\nexport function estimateCreditsFromSize(sizeBytes: number, options: CreditEstimateOptions = {}): number {\n  const estimatedSeconds = estimateAudioSecondsFromSize(sizeBytes);\n\n  if (options.costEstimators?.length) {\n    const maxEstimatedCost = Math.max(\n      ...options.costEstimators.map((estimateCost) => estimateCost(estimatedSeconds))\n    );\n    return Math.max(0.1, creditsForCost(maxEstimatedCost));\n  }\n\n  const estimatedCredits = (estimatedSeconds / 60) * CREDITS_PER_MINUTE;\n  return Math.max(0.1, roundUpToTenth(estimatedCredits));\n}\n\nexport async function validateCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  estimatedCredits: number,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<CreditsResult> {\n  const balance = roundToTenth(auth.credits);\n  if (balance < estimatedCredits) {\n    return { ok: false, response: insufficientCreditsResponse(balance, estimatedCredits) };\n  }\n  return { ok: true };\n}\n\nasync function recordLicenseUsage(\n  licenseKey: string,\n  creditsUsed: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>\n): Promise<void> {\n  const apiBase = (process.env.NEXTJS_LICENSE_API_URL || DEFAULT_API_BASE_URL).replace(/\\/+$/, '');\n\n  try {\n    const response = await fetch(`${apiBase}/api/license/credits`, {\n      method: 'POST',\n      headers: {\n        'Content-Type': 'application/json',\n      },\n      body: JSON.stringify({\n        license_key: licenseKey,\n        amount: creditsUsed,\n        metadata,\n      }),\n      signal: AbortSignal.timeout(LICENSE_API_TIMEOUT_MS),\n    });\n\n    if (!response.ok) {\n      const errorData = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));\n      console.warn('POST /api/license/credits failed', {\n        status: response.status,\n        error: (errorData as Record<string, unknown>).error || 'Unknown error',\n        creditsUsed,\n      });\n      return;\n    }\n\n    const data = await response.json() as { credits_remaining?: number; credits_deducted?: number };\n    if (typeof data.credits_remaining === 'number') {\n      await cacheLicense(licenseKey, {\n        isValid: true,\n        credits: data.credits_remaining,\n        cachedAt: new Date().toISOString(),\n      });\n    }\n  } catch (error) {\n    console.warn('POST /api/license/credits network error', {\n      error: error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error),\n    });\n  }\n}\n\n// In-flight deduction tracking for graceful shutdown.\n// Call sites fire deductCredits() without awaiting (response latency), so a\n// Fly machine recycle (SIGTERM on deploy/scale-down) between the response\n// flush and the redis/license write would silently drop the charge. Every\n// deduction registers here so the SIGTERM handler can drain before exit.\nconst inFlightDeductions = new Set<Promise<number>>();\n\nexport async function drainPendingDeductions(timeoutMs: number): Promise<number> {\n  const pendingCount = inFlightDeductions.size;\n  if (pendingCount === 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  const allSettled = Promise.allSettled([...inFlightDeductions]);\n  const timeout = new Promise<void>((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, timeoutMs));\n  await Promise.race([allSettled, timeout]);\n  return pendingCount;\n}\n\nexport function deductCredits(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const deduction = performDeduction(auth, costUsd, metadata, clientIP);\n  inFlightDeductions.add(deduction);\n  deduction\n    .catch(() => {}) // errors are logged inside performDeduction / by callers\n    .finally(() => inFlightDeductions.delete(deduction));\n  return deduction;\n}\n\nasync function performDeduction(\n  auth: AuthContext,\n  costUsd: number,\n  metadata: Record<string, unknown>,\n  _clientIP: string\n): Promise<number> {\n  const creditsUsed = creditsForCost(costUsd);\n\n  if (creditsUsed <= 0) {\n    return 0;\n  }\n\n  await recordLicenseUsage(auth.identifier, creditsUsed, metadata);\n  return creditsUsed;\n}\n","numLines":147,"startLine":1,"totalLines":147}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"282e7769-dd35-418f-9361-958bb03a5d03","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"/Users/ray/Desktop/ray-os","sessionId":"0ad0c358-bd4c-4ff8-872f-2f81b1b9e66c","version":"2.1.220","gitBranch":"main","slug":"lively-giggling-hammock"}

Here are my findings.\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/src/lib/license-key-redirect.ts`\nLine: 13-20\nInvariant: I13\nClaim: `sanitizeReturnTo` allow-lists on `startsWith(\"/\")` + `!startsWith(\"//\")` + `!startsWith(\"/\\\\\")`, but the WHATWG URL parser strips tab/CR/LF from a URL *before* resolving it, so `\"/\\t/evil.com\"` passes the check and then resolves to an off-origin URL.\nFailure scenario: A signed-in user (session cookie present) who clicks `https://hyperwhisper.com/en/user/sign-in?returnTo=%2F%09%2Fevil.com` gets a 307 from `nextjs/middleware.ts:137` (`NextResponse.redirect(new URL(redirectUrl, request.url))`) to `https://evil.com/` — an open redirect from the real product domain, usable for phishing a license key or magic-link. Verified in Node: `new URL(\"/\\t/evil.com\", \"https://hyperwhisper.com/\")` → `https://evil.com/`; `\\n` and `\\r` behave identically. The same helper feeds `getCallbackURL()` in `app/[locale]/user/sign-in/page.tsx:44` (→ `router.push(data.redirect)` at line 71) and `sanitizeLicenseKeyRedirect` in `src/lib/auth-license-key-plugin.ts:84`, so the license-key and magic-link post-auth redirects share the bypass.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/app/[locale]/blog/[slug]/page.tsx`\nLine: 170\nInvariant: I13\nClaim: `post.html` is rendered with `dangerouslySetInnerHTML` with no sanitizer anywhere in the chain; it is `blog_posts.content_html`, written verbatim from the Outrank webhook's `article.content_html` (`app/api/webhooks/add-blog-post/route.ts:310, 353, 371`) and read back unchanged (`src/content/blog.ts:51`).\nFailure scenario: Outrank (or anyone holding the static `OUTRANK_WEBHOOK_TOKEN` bearer, which the file's own header comment calls a permanent, non-rotating shared secret) publishes one article whose `content_html` contains `<img src=x onerror=...>`. `export const revalidate = 60` + `force-static` means the poisoned HTML is baked into the cached page and served to every visitor of `https://hyperwhisper.com/en/blog/<slug>`. Same-origin script then drives `/api/customer/profile` and the `customer.*` tRPC procedures with the visitor's HttpOnly session cookie and exfiltrates their Account Keys and credit balances. Note the route already has a correct escaping path — `markdownToHtml()` at line 172-190 escapes — so only the `content_html` branch is unsafe.\nConfidence: high\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/post-process.ts`\nLine: 77\nInvariant: I13\nClaim: `body = await c.req.json()` buffers and parses the whole request body with no `Content-Length` gate, and it runs *before* `validateAuth` (line 111). The `MAX_TEXT_LENGTH` (100 000 chars) check at line 87 only fires after the allocation. `src/index.ts` registers no body-limit middleware, and `export default { port, fetch, websocket }` never sets `maxRequestBodySize`, so Bun's 128 MB default is the only bound.\nFailure scenario: An unauthenticated caller (only the `isIPBlocked` Redis check precedes it) POSTs `Content-Type: application/json` bodies of ~128 MB to `/post-process`. Each in-flight request holds the raw bytes plus the UTF-16 string plus the parsed object — several hundred MB — on a machine configured at `memory = '1gb'` (`fly.prod.toml:24`). A few concurrent requests OOM-kill the Bun process, taking down `/transcribe` for every paying user in that region, and any credit deductions still in `inFlightDeductions` are lost with it (no SIGTERM, so `gracefulShutdown` never drains). Both sibling routes get this right — `transcribe.ts:219-231` and `assistant.ts:255` gate on `Content-Length` before touching the body — so `/post-process` is the odd one out.\nConfidence: medium\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `hyperwhisper-cloud/src/routes/usage.ts`\nLine: 49 (via `lib/redis.ts:72`)\nInvariant: I13\nClaim: The `account_key` / `license_key` / `identifier` query value is never format- or length-checked before it is used as a Redis key (`license:${licenseKey}`), and `validateLicenseAndGetCredits` calls `cacheLicense` unconditionally — including for keys the licensing API just rejected.\nFailure scenario: `GET /usage` is unauthenticated and has no rate limiter. An attacker loops `GET /usage?license_key=<random>`; every miss writes a fresh `license:<random>` entry into Upstash with a 3600 s TTL. Thousands of requests per minute inflate the Upstash command/storage bill and, once the plan's memory ceiling is hit, evict genuine `license:<key>` entries — every evicted paying customer's next transcription round-trips to `/api/license/validate` instead of hitting cache, adding latency and load to the Vercel licensing API. (`middleware/auth.ts` is not affected on the same path: it caches only for non-5xx, but it also does no key validation.)\nConfidence: medium\n\n### Candidate\nFile: `nextjs/app/[locale]/layout.tsx`\nLine: 55-62 (with `src/i18n/locales.ts:120-129`)\nInvariant: I13\nClaim: `generateMetadata` reads `headersList.get(\"x-pathname\")` from `next/headers` — i.e. the *incoming request* headers — and interpolates it into `alternates.canonical`, every `alternates.languages[locale]` entry, and `openGraph.url`. `middleware.ts` sets `x-pathname` on the **response** (`response.headers.set`, lines 108/127/141/147), so it never reaches the request; the only source of this header is the client.\nFailure scenario: A caller who can inject a request header (a misbehaving intermediary proxy, or a cache-poisoning primitive against Vercel's edge cache, since `x-pathname` is not part of the cache key) sends `x-pathname: /en/@evil.com` and the rendered page emits `<link rel=\"canonical\" href=\"https://hyperwhisper.com/en/@evil.com\">` plus 40 `hreflang` alternates pointing at the same. If the poisoned render is cached, every subsequent visitor to that path is served attacker-chosen canonical/alternate URLs, handing search-engine authority away from the real page. React escapes the attribute so this is SEO/cache poisoning, not script execution.\nConfidence: low\n\n### Coverage\n\n**Examined buckets:** B1 (cloud entry/auth/redis/query/constants), B2 (all five cloud routes end to end), B5 partial (constants, cost gates, `gcs-storage` URL construction), B6 (all of `api/license/*`, `api/account/*`, `api/checkout/*`, both webhooks), B7 (`api/internal/*`, `api/config`, `api/customer/profile`, `api/download`, `middleware.ts`, `src/lib/{auth-license-key-plugin,license-key-redirect}.ts`, `download-ip.ts`), B8 (grep-level over every `*Procedure` + zod input schema in `server/api/routers/**`), B9 (all four email templates + `escape-html.ts`, `services/{email,geolocation}.ts`, raw-SQL sites in `db-layer.ts`), B10 partial (blog list/detail, `src/content/blog.ts`, `[locale]/layout.tsx`, `src/i18n/{request,routing,locales}.ts`, `i18n.ts`), B11 partial (`sign-in`, `credits`, `purchase-success`, `auth/sign-out`).\n\n**Not examined / partially examined:**\n- B3 (cloud STT providers) and B4 (cloud LLM providers) — swept only for URL/template interpolation of caller-controlled values (`grep` for `` fetch(` ``, `` URL(` ``, `https://.*${`).
